# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Terr (Mois de ṭerr)

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 45  
**Columns**: pp. 8–246  

[p. 8] {#po.45.p8}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። ወርኃ ፡ጥር፣ ቡሩክ ፣ መዓልቱ ፡ ፲ሰዓት ፤ ወእምዝ ፡ ይትዌሰክ፤ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. The month of terr (is) blessed. Its (first) day (is) of ten hours; then (the days) increase.

### READING OF THE FIRST TERR
አመ ፡ ፳ለጥር ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ኮነ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ዲያቆናት ፣ ወቀዳሜ ፤ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ዘመጽሐፈ፤ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ኮነ" ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በእንቲ አሁ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወኀይለ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፤ በውስተ ፡፤ ሕዝብ ። ወቀንኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አይሁድ ፡፣ ወመሰጥዎ፥ ፡ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምኩራቦሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ረከብናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፤ ብእሲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸርፍ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ይዌልጥ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወይነሥቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ 'መካን ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ዓውደ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ ለእስጢፋኖስ ፡ ወገጹ ፡ ከመ ፡ገጸ፣ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።፡ "ወሶበ ፡ ይቤልዎ፤ እመንኑ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘተብህሉ ፡ በእንቲአከ ። ወአውሥኦሙ ፡ በቃል ፡ ፍጹም፤፤ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ወአኃዊነ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ በማእከለ ፡ አፍላግ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይፍልስ ፡ እምካራን፤ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ፃእ ፡ እምብሔርከ፤ ፡ ወእምአዝማዲከ ፡ ወናዓ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ዘአነ ፡ ኣርአየከ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምካራን ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ እግዚአብሔር! ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተናገሮሙ!፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለይስሐቅ ፡ ወበእ ንተ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወደቂቁ ። ወዘከመ ፡ ሜጥዎ ፡'ለዮሴፍ ፡ አኃዊሁ! ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ አመሮሙ፤ ፡ ርእሶ፤፤ ፡ ለአኀዊሁ! ። ወተናገሮሙ?" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ እስከ ፡ "በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጊዜ።፤ ፡ ሕንጸቱ ፡ ለቤተ ፣ መቅደስ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ሐተመ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፣ ግዙፍ፤ ፡ ክሳድክሙ ፡ ወቄላፍ ፡ አልባቢክሙ ፡ አንትሙሰ ፡ 'በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ትትቃረንዎ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በከመ ፡ አበዊክሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ሰደድዎሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፡ እለ ፡ ተነበዩ ፡ ወሰበኩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ዘአንትሙ ፡ ሰቀልክ ምዎ፤" ፡ ወተንሥአ ፤ እምውታን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዕዎ፥" ፡ ሐቀዩ ፡ ስነኒሆሙ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነጸረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፣ እንዘ ፡
On this day the apostle 'Estifänos (= Stephen), archdeacon and protomartyr, became a martyr. (This is) that saint about whom the book of the Acts of the apostles testifies that he was full of grace, of Holy Spirit, and of power and performed miracles and wonders among the people. The Jews were jealous of him, took him, and brought him into their temple saying: "We have found this man blaspheming God and Moses and he has said that Jesus would change the law of Moses and overturn this holy place." Then, those who were sitting in the enclosure of the tribunal looked at 'Estifänos, and his face (was) like the face of an angel of God. When they said to him: "Are the words that have been said about you accurate?" he answered them with a full voice and said to them: "Listen, (you) our fathers and our brothers, the God of glory appeared to our father Abrehäm (= Abraham) in the middle of the rivers (= in Mesopotamia) before (the latter) left Kärän (= Haran) and he said to him: 'Go out of your country and from your kindred and go into the land that I, I will show you.' And ('Abrehäm) went out of Kärän as the Lord had ordered him." Then ('Estifänos) spoke to them about the birth of Yeshaq (= Isaac), of Yä'qob (= Jacob), and of his sons. (He told them) how his brothers had sold Yosëf (= Joseph) and how (the latter) showed himself to his brothers. The saint 'Estifänos spoke to them until he arrived at the moment of the construction of the Temple. Then he concluded his speech and said to them: "Stiff (is) your neck and uncircumcised your heart. You, at all times, you resist the Holy Spirit like your fathers who persecuted the prophets who prophesied and preached the coming of Christ whom you, you have crucified and (who) is risen from the dead!" When they heard him, they gnashed their teeth against him. But the saint 'Estifanos was full of faith and of Holy Spirit, he looked to heaven and saw the glory of God and Jesus Christ standing at the right hand of God, and he said to them: "Behold I see the heavens opened and Jesus is standing at the right hand of God." When they heard him, they stopped their ears and took him to stone him. He


[p. 10] {#po.45.p10}
ይቀውም ፡ በየማነ ፡ እግዚአብሔርቶ፥፤ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፡ እሬኢ ፡ ለሰማያት ፡ ርኅወ ፡ ወኢየሱስ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ በየማነ፥ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዕዎ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ እዘኒሆሙ፥ ፡ ወነሥእዎቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግርዎ ፡ በአእባንቶ ። ወሰገደ ፡ በብረኪሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ተወከፍ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ኀቤከ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይዌግርዎ ፡ በአአባን ፡ ሰአለ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ኢትጐልቀ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ዛተ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ተመሰለ ፡ በእግዚኡቶ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ዘንተ ፡ አዕረፈ" ፡
knelt down saying: "Lord, receive my soul unto yourself." While they were stoning him, he prayed to God for them and said: "Lord, do not count this sin against them." He resembled (thus) his Lord. When he had said this, he died. Believing men came, carried away his holy body, made a great lamentation over him, and buried him. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

On this day also the saint Läwdyänos (= Leontius) became a martyr. This saint was in the time of Maksimiyänos (= Maximian), the impious emperor, in the land of Soryä (= Syria). When (the emperor) learned about this saint that he was a fighter (spiritual) and worshipped God, he sent and brought him before him; he offered him much money and undertook to seduce him so that he might abandon the worship of Christ and worship his (= the emperor's) idols. But the saint Läwndyänos laughed at him and disdained his gifts, his money, and his honors; he despised his tortures, cursed him, and cursed his impure idols. Then (the emperor) became greatly angry against him and ordered him to be suspended on a wheel and to make him undergo a great torture, and they did to him as the emperor had ordered. God brought him out of the torture unharmed, without damage. Then, (the emperor) ordered him to be broken with clubs, to boil oil and grease in a large cauldron, and to throw the saint into it, and they did that to him. In all (this) torture he was constant and our Lord Christ strengthened him and raised him up unharmed, without damage. When (the emperor) was weary of him and of torturing him, he ordered his head to be cut off. They cut off his head with a sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Many miracles and wonders appeared from his body; his reputation was known throughout the land of Soryä and they built churches and monasteries for him. In one of his monasteries was baptized from his childhood our holy father saint abbä Säwiros (= Severus), patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch). May the prayer and the blessing of this saint Läwndyänos be with us, amen.
አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡፣ መቃርዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጸጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖፕልቆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፳ወ፻ ። 'ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእምቅድሜሁ ፡ ተጋ
On this day also died the holy father abbä Magäryos (= Macarius), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the number of the patriarchs, he is the sixty-ninth. As for this father, when died abbä Mikä'ël (= Michael), the patriarch who (was) before him, the bishops, the elders, and the


[p. 12] {#po.45.p12}
ብኡ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆጳሳት ፡ 'ወሊቃውንት ፡ ወሊሂቃነ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወዓርጉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኀበ" ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወነበሩ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእሉ፣ "ወየሐትቱ ፤ እምገዳማዊያን'"" ፡ ወጻድቃን ፡ ወእለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 'በዓታት ፡ ወዖማዕታት?፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘይቴይስ” ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሚመት ፡ ክብርት ። ወነገሮሙ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ጻድቅ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ በደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቴይስ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሚመት"፣ ወአኀዝዎ"" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ወአሰርዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸርኅ ፡ ወይብል ፡ አነ ፡ አባሲ ፡ "ወኢይበቅሞዕ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወኢሰምዑ ፡ እምኔሁ?" ፡ አላ ፡ ሜጫምዎ ፡ ሊቀ፣ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ። ወውእቱሰ ፤ ጫመ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳተ ፡ ወካህናተ ፣ በኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወሐደሰ ፤ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናተብዙኃተ” ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ሕዝበ።፣ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአብያተ፣0ክርስቲያናት'-፤ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡'በዳኅና፤ ወበሰላም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሐዘን ። ወነበረ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ ፳ወዒዓመተ ፡ጣወ፳ዕለተ'፣ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወትንብልናሁ ፡
presbyters of Egypt gathered, all went up to the desert of 'Asqëtes (= Scetis) and took place in the monastery of the saint abbä Magäres (= Macarius) by questioning and inquiring from the solitaries, (the) righteous, and (those) who dwelt in caves and cells of who was the best for this illustrious charge. A righteous man spoke to them about this saint and said to them: "The priest Magäres who dwells in the monastery of the saint abbä Magäres, he is the best for this charge." They seized him against his will and bound him while he was crying and saying: "I (am) a sinner and am not fit for this work." But they did not listen to him and they consecrated him patriarch against his will. As for him, he consecrated bishops and priests throughout the land of Egypt. || He restored many churches, and all the people of the Christians and the churches were in his time in salvation and peace without affliction. He remained in his charge twenty-seven years (and) forty-one days, he pleased God and died in peace. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us, amen.
የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ሰማዕታተ ፡ አክሚም ። ወከመዝ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ሀሎ ፡ 8ብእሲ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አክሚም ፡ መስፍን ፡ ወባዕል ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወበብሩር ፡ ወስሙ ፣ እልሲድልማዮስ ፡ ወወለደ ፡ ፪ደቂቀ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ዲዮስቆ ሮስ ፡ ወሰከላብዮስ ፡ ወተሐፅኑ ፡ በፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ፈተዉዳ ፣ አልባሰ ፣ ምንኩስና ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ይልበሱ ፡ ወአስተርአዮሙ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ደብረ ፡አባ፣፡ሙሴ፣ ገዳማዊ ፤ ወሐዊሮሙ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ለብሱ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ በተጋድሎ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ወመንክረ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋፅዕል" ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ወሰከላብዮስ ፡ ዲያቆነ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ክህዶ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ለአምላከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ለአርያኖስ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ አንጽና ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትል ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ዘኢፇዑ ፡ ለአማልክቲሁ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ብሔረጾ ፡ አክሚም ፡ ወአኃዞ ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብኑድ ዮስ ፡ ወአሰሮ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሩ ። ወአስተርአዮሙ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስቦ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ለዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ወለሰከላብዮስ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወ፳ቿለወርኃ ፡ ታኅሣሥ ፡ ከዊ ኖሙ ፤ ፳ወ፪መነኮሳተ ፡ ወበጺሖሙ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አክሚም ፡፣ ረከብዎሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ተጋቢኦሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አንስቲያሆሙ ፡ ወደቂቆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘእግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኃኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፤ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይግበሩ ፡ በዓለ ፤ ልደት ፡ ስቡሕ ፤ ወይሙቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ብኑድዮስ ፡ ኤዲጺስ ፡ ቆይስ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ። ወበሳኒታሁ ፡ ቀደሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ብኑድዮስ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይብል ፡ አግዮስ ፡ ጸርሑ ፡ መላእክት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ”፡ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
[On this day also were martyrs the holy martyrs of 'Akmim (= Akhmim), thus is their story: there was a man among the inhabitants of 'Akmim, (he was) a judge, rich in gold and silver, his name (was) 'Elsidlemäyos, he had begotten two sons whose names (were) Diyosqoros (= Dioscorus) and Sakaläbyos (= Aesculapius). They were raised in the fear of God, in fasting and in prayer. When their father died, they desired to put on the monastic garment and an angel of God appeared to them (and told them) to go to the monastery of abbä Musë (= Moses) the solitary. Having gone to him, they put on the monastic garment and dwelt in a great combat (spiritual) by performing wonders and miracles. Some time later, Diyosqoros was ordained priest and Sakaläbyos, deacon. Then, when Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) had denied the God of heaven, he ordered 'Aryänos (= Arianus), governor of 'Ansenä (= Antinoë), to kill the Christians who did not sacrifice to his gods. 'Aryänos arrived in the region of 'Akmim, apprehended the bishop abbä Benudyos (= Abadion), bound him, and entered his city. The angel of God, saint Mikä'el (= Michael) appeared to Diyosqoros and to Sakaläbyos and told them that they would receive the crown of martyrdom. They went to 'Aryänos on the twenty-eighth of the month of tähsäs, twenty-four monks being (with them). Arrived at the city of 'Akmim, they found the people of the Christians gathered with their wives and their children in the church of our Lord and our Savior Jesus Christ - glory to him! - to celebrate the feast of the glorious Nativity and to die for his (= Christ's) name; abbä Benudyos the bishop entered with them. The next day, the saint abbä Benudyos officiated and, when he arrived where one says:


[p. 14] {#po.45.p14}
ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፀባኦት ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ምሉእ ፡ ሰማያተ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ቅድሳተ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ። ወርእይዎ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ለእግዚእነ” ፡ ነቢሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ወመላእክት ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ዓውዶ ፡ ወይነሥእ ፡ ምሦርባነ ፡ ወያነብሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደ ፡ ካህን ፡ ወያቄር ቦሙ ፡ ለጉቡኣን ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ መዓተ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፤ ክርስቲያን ፣፤ ወአኃዞሙ ፡ ለ፪ሊቃውንተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘስሞሙ ፡፣ ብሕምሁፋ ፡ ወውኒን ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወድኅሬሆሙ ፡ ቀተለ ፡ ዲያቆናተ ፡ ወንፍቀ ፡ ዲያቆናተ ፣፤ ወመዘምራነ ፤ ወመገብተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወኢያትረፈ ፡ አንስተ ፡ ወደቀ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ እምአንቀጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወውኅዘ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፳እመት ። ወእምዝ ፤ ነሥእዎሙ"" ፡፣ ለአባ ፣ ብኑድዮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆቆስ ፡ ወለዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ወለሰከላብዮስ ፡ ወለአኃው ፡ እለ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወወሰድዎሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ወጌጦሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግዱ ፡ ለጣዖታት ። ወሶበ ፡ ዓበይዎ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይዝብጥዎሙ ፡ ለዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ወለሰከላብዮስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተሌለያ ፤ አዕጽምቲሆሙ ወአስተርአዮሙ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ወአሕየዎሙ ። ወርእዮሙ ፡፣ ኮርዮን ፤ ወፊልሞን ፡ ዐበይተ ፡ ሐራሁ ፡ ለአርያኖስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ወጓሐራ ፡ አለዎ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፤"" ። ወአዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውርውዎሙ ፡ ውስተ!" ፡ እሳት ፡ ወፈ ጸሙ ፥፤ ስምዖሙ ፡ አመ ፡ በለታኅሣሥ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ወረዉ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶነ ፡ እሳት ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘያጌብሮሙ ፣ ወፈጸሙ ፡ ስምዖሙ ።
"Holy" ('agyos), the angels cried out saying: "Holy, holy, holy (is) the God of hosts, the holiness of your glory fills completely the heavens and the earth." The saints saw our Lord standing on the altar, the angels were standing around him, he took the Eucharist, placed it in the hand of the priest, and (the latter) presented it to the (faithful) assembled. Having learned (this), 'Aryänos was taken with a great anger, entered the church, (had) seized two elders of the city whose names (were) Behmehufa and Wenin and (had) their heads cut off with a sword. After them, he (had) killed the deacons, the subdeacons, the cantors, and the stewards of the church: he did not spare the women nor the children, so much so that their blood came out of the entrance of the church and flowed for twenty cubits. Then they took abbä Benudyos the bishop, Diyosqoros, Sakaläbyos, and the brothers who (were) with them, they brought them to 'Aryänos and (the latter) made them promises so that they might worship the idols. When they had refused (it) to him, he ordered to strike Diyosqoros and Sakaläbyos until their bones separated. The angel of the Most High God appeared to the saints and healed them. Seeing this miracle, Koryon and Filmon (= Philemon), officers of the soldiers of 'Aryänos, and forty soldiers who (were) with them, believed in our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! The governor ordered to throw them into the fire, and they finished their martyrdom on the thirtieth of tähsäs; many threw themselves into the furnace of fire without anyone forcing them and finished their martyrdom.
ወእምዝ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለጥር ፡ እንዘ ፤ ሀለዉ ፡ ሙቁሓነ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ወሰከላብዮስ ፡ አስተርአዮሙ ፤ ቅዱስ፤"" ፣ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡፣ መላእክት ፣፤ ወአጥብፆሙ!" ። ወበሳኒታሁ ፡ ተናገሮሙ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰጊድ ፡ ለጣዖት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አበይዎ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይምልሑ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ለዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ወነሚኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፤ ሜጦሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካኖሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወርእዮ ፡ ሉክያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ አምነ ፡ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ 'በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት!" ፡ ወወገርዎሙ፤" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግብ ፡ ወፈጸሙ ፡ ስምዖሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ" ፡ አርያ ኖስ ፡ ይቅትልዎሙ ፡ ለ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይጹልዩ ፡ አስተርአዮሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፤ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይጹውዕ ፡ ስሞሙ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ወይጽሕፍ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ይጐል ቆሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ሐራዊ ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰ ይፍ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ወለሰከላብዮስ፤የ ፡ መተርዎ፤! ፡ ሐቋሁ ፡ ወለ፳ወ፪መነ ኮሳት ፣ ሠጠቅዎሙ ፡ እምነ ፤ ቆሞሙ ፡ ወተከለሉ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለጥር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገነዝዎሙ ፤ ሥጋሆሙ ፡፣ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዘአዝማዲሆሙ ፡፣ ወቀበሮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘተቀደሰት ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አምላክ፤!፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ጐልቆሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ተቀተሉ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ፡፻ወዓ፤፤። ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Then, the first of terr, while Diyosqoros and Sakaläbyos were imprisoned, the saint Mikä'ël the archangel appeared to them and comforted them. The next day, 'Aryänos spoke to the saints about the worship of idols; when they had refused him, he ordered to tear out the eyes of Diyosqoros; having taken his eyes, the saint put them back in their place, they were as before. Seeing (this), Lukyänos (= Lucianus) an officer believed with his soldiers in our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! They threw them into a pit and they finished their martyrdom. Then 'Aryänos ordered to kill the saints; while (the latter) were praying, our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! - appeared to them and gave them a covenant for whoever would invoke their name, would make their commemoration, and would write the book of their combat (spiritual) in order to count it with the assembly of the saints. Then a soldier approached and cut off with a sword the head of the saint Diyosqoros, and to Sakaläbyos they severed the loins. As for the twenty-four monks, they split them in the height. They were crowned (with the crown of martyrdom) on the first of terr. Then Sämu'el, who (was) of their relatives, shrouded their bodies and buried them in the church which had been consecrated in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. The number of the martyrs who were killed is 8040. May their holy blessing be with our king 'lyäsu in the centuries of centuries.]


[p. 16] {#po.45.p16}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡፡'እለ ፡ ሞቱ፤፤" ፡ ትማልም ፡ ወዝውእቱ ፡ ዘቀተሎሙ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ጐልቆሙ ፡ፓቋካህናት ፡፻ወ በዲያቆ ናት ፡ ዛወጀመዘምራን ፤ ወመገብተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን!፤፤ ፡ ፻ወ ፃንፍቀ ፡ ዲያቆ ናት ፤ ወሕዝባውያን ፡ :፻ወ8፻ወ8፪፤፤! ወሊቃውንቲሆሙ ፡ ልድያኖስ ፡ ወታድርስ ፡ ወጳውሎስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀ ሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ!! ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also (others) were martyrs - apart from those who had died the day before, that is to say those whom Diyogletyänos killed. Their number is sixty priests, one hundred thirty deacons, fifty-three cantors, eighty church stewards, one hundred fifty subdeacons, eight thousand four hundred ninety laymen and their elders Ledyänos (= Lydianus), Tädres (= Theodore), and Päwlos (= Paul). May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear Za-Manfas Qeddus in the centuries of centuries.]

### READING OF THE 2 TERR
አመ ፡ ፪ለጥር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፤ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቤል ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ዘተክዕወ ፡ ደሙ ፡ በዓመፃ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ በኩረ ፡ ምውታን ፡ ዘቀተሎ ፡ ቃየል ፤ እጉሁ ። ወምክንያቱሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዓለወ ፡ አዳም ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበልዓ ፡ እምፅፅ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢይብላዕ ፡ ዘተአዘዘ ፤ ተሠልጠ ፤ ሞት ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ውሉዱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ለኃጢአት ፡ ወተበውሐ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለአስሕቶ ፡ ዓለም ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምገነት ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ታሕቲት ፡ ነበረ ፡ ፻ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ 'ይላሑ ፡፤ ወይበኪ፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘተዓርቀ ፡ እምስብሐተ ፡ ክብሩ ፡ ወተሰደ ፡ እምርስቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ላሁ ፡ አእመራ ፡ ለሔ ዋን ፡ ወፀንሰት ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወወለደቶ፤ ፡ ለቃየል ፡ ወለሉድቶ ፡ እኅቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አእመራ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ወወለደቶ ፡ ለአቤል ፡ ወለአቅሊማ ፡ እኅቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ወርዘዉ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ አዳም ፡ ለሔዋን ፡ ናሁ ፡ ወርዘዉ ፡ እሉ ፡ ደቂቅኪ ፡ ለያውስባ ፡ እንከ ፡ ቃየል ፤ ለአቅሊማ ፡ ዘተወልደት ፡ "ምስለ ፡ አቤል ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ለያውስባ ፡ አቤል ፡ ለሉድ ፡ ዘተወልደት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቃየል ። ወይቤላ ፡ ቃየል ፡ ለሔዋን ፡ እሙ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ እንከ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንሣእ ፡ እኅትየ ፡ ዘተወልደት ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ወአቤልኒ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ እኅቶ ፡ ዘተወልደት ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወዝኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሉድ ፡ ዘተወልደት ፡ምስለ ፡ ቃየል ፡ኮነት፣ ትሜኒ ፤ እምአቅሊማ ፡ እኅተ ፡ አቤል ፡ ወትመስል ፡ ሔዋንሃ ፡ እማ ። ወ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አዳም ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ዓፀቦ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዝንቱሰ ፡ ውፁዕ ፡ እምሕግ ፣፡ ወኢይደል ወከ ፡ ትግበር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወትንሣእ ፡ እኅተከ ፡ ዘተወልደት ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ ቦአ ፤ ቅንዓት ፤ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ቃየል ፡፤ ወፈቀደ ፡ ቀቲሎቶ ፡ ለአቤል ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ አዳም ፡ ለቃየል ፡ ወለአቤል ፡ ሑሩ ፡ ወአዕረጉ ፡ ዮርባነ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዘሠምረ ፡ 'ለይኩን ፡ ውእቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አዕረጉ ፡ ቱርባኖሙ ፡ ነጸረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ መሥዋዕተ ፡ አቤል ፡ ወተወከፈ ፡ ፉርባኖ ፡ ወመነነ ፡ ምርባነ ፡ ቃየል ፡ ወአሜሃ ፡ ተወሰከ ፡ ቅንዓት ፡ ወመዓት ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኃሠሠ ፡ ምክን ያተ ፤ በዘይቅትሎ ። ወሶበ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቄላ ፡ ተራከቦ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለቃየል ፡ "ወይቤሎ ፡ ምንት ፡ ያኀዝነከ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ቃየል ፡ እስመ ፡ አዘዘኒ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ከመ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day died 'Abël (= Abel) the righteous whose blood was shed with injustice; he is the first-born of the dead whom Qäyal (= Cain) his brother killed. The cause of this (is the following): when 'Adäm (= Adam) transgressed the commandment of God and ate of the tree of which it had been ordered to him not to eat, death had power over him and over his children; he became a slave to sin and Satan had license to deceive the world. Then, when ('Adäm) went out of the garden and descended to the lower earth, he remained one hundred years lamenting and weeping because of the fact that he had been deprived of his glorious honors and driven from his inheritance. When he had finished his lamentation, he knew Hëwän (= Eve); she conceived from him and gave birth to Qäyal and Lud (= Eliud) his (twin) sister. Then ('Adäm) knew her again and she gave birth for him to 'Abël and 'Aglimä (= Aqlima) his (twin) sister. When his children had become adolescents, 'Adäm said to Hëwän: "Behold these your children have become adolescents, let therefore Qäyal marry 'Aqlimä who was born with 'Abël and that also 'Abël marry Lud who was born with Qäyal." Qäyal said to Hëwän his mother: "It is fitting that I take my (twin) sister who was born with me and that 'Abël take his (twin) sister who was born with him." This (was said) because Lud who was born with Qäyal was more beautiful than 'Aqlimä the (twin) sister of 'Abël, and she resembled Hëwän her mother. When 'Adäm heard these words, it was very painful to him and he said: "This is contrary to the law. It is not fitting for you to do this and to take your (twin) sister who was born with you." At that moment, jealousy entered into the heart of Qäyal and he wanted to kill 'Abël. Then 'Adäm said to Qäyal and to 'Abël: "Go and offer a sacrifice to God. The one whom he will accept, may he be (accepted?)." Then, when they offered their sacrifices, God looked at the offering of 'Abël and accepted his sacrifice and he disdained the sacrifice of Qäyal. Then the jealousy and the anger (of the latter) redoubled, Satan entered into him and he sought a pretext to kill him (= 'Abël). When he went down into the valley, Satan met Qäyal and said to him: "What is it that afflicts you?" Qäyal said to him: "My father has ordered me to give my beautiful (and) pretty sister to my brother 'Abel." Satan said to him: "Listen to my word, Qäyal my friend, (you) who listen to

አሀብ ፡ እኅትየ፤‹ ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ ላህይ ፡ ለእጥየ ፡ አቤል ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ስማዕ ፡ ቃልየ ፡'ኦቃየል ፡ አርክየ፤፤ ፡ ስማዔ ፡ ምዕዳን ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ዴግኖ ፡ ለእቱከ ፡ ወተራከቦ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰቲ ፡ ወዝብጥ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በእብን ። ወሶበ ፡ ይመውት ፡ ታወስቦን ፡ ለክልኤሆን ፡ አሐቲከ ። ወአደሞ ፡ ለቃየል ፡ ምክረ፤ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወነደ ፡ በፍትወተ ፡ ዝሙት ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ኮነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሐጐል ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ እብነ ፡ ወዘበጠ! ፡ ክሳዶ ፣ ለአቤል ፤ወሞተ ፡ አቤል፤ ፡ በእደ ፡ እጉሁ ፡ በቅንዓተ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ወዝውእቱ ፡ጥንተ፣፤
the admonition, arise, follow your brother, meet him while he is drinking and strike his head with a stone. When he is dead, you will marry your two sisters." The advice of Satan pleased Qäyal and he burned with the flame of lust, for he was the son of perdition. Then, he took a stone, struck the neck of 'Abël and 'Abël died by the hand of his brother because of jealousy for a woman. This was the beginning of affliction and the beginning of death in the world.
ኃዘን ፡ ወጥንተ ፡ ሞት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወለሔዋን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፤ ኢ ትብልዑ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ እስመ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ትበልዑ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ትመውቱ ፡ ወዝቃል ፡ ቀደመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አቤል ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለቃየል ፡ አይቴ ፡ሀሎ ፡ አቤል ፡ እቱከ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ቃየን፤፤ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ 'ዓቃቤሁ ፡ አነ፤ ለአቤል ፡ እጉየ፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቃለ ፡ ደሙ ፡ ለእቱከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ርግምተ፣ ትኩን ፡ ምድር ፡ እንተ ፡ አብቀወት ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስተይ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ለእቱከ ፡ ርዑደ ፡ ወድንጉፀ ፡ ኩን! ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወትከ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ መርገም ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ቃየል ፤ በዝኃ" ፡ እስከ ፡ ተደምሰሰ ፡ ዘርኡ ፡ እምገጸ ፡ ምድር ፡ በማየ ፡ አይኅ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ 'አቤል ፤ ዘተክዕወ ፡ ደሙ፥ ።
Because of this, God said to Adam and Eve while he was in the garden: "Do not eat of the tree, for on the day you eat of it, you shall surely die." This word (was fulfilled) first against Abel. God said to Cain: "Where is Abel, your brother?" Cain said to him: "Am I the keeper of Abel, my brother?" God said to him: "The voice of your brother's blood has reached me; cursed be the earth that has opened its mouth to drink the blood of your brother. Be trembling and fearful all the days of your life." This curse was very great against Cain until his offspring was wiped from the face of the earth by the water of the flood, because of Abel whose blood had been shed.
ወሄኖክኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸርሕ ፡ ደሙ ፡ ለአቤል ፡ ወይሰክዮ ፡ ለእጉሁ ። ወአበዊነሂ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይምሕሉ ፡ በደመ ፡ አቤል ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ማየ ፡ አይኅ ። ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለጸሐፍት ፡ ወፈሪሳውያን ፡ መነ ፡ እምነቢያት ፡ ዘኢቀተሉ ፡ አበዊክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ፡፣ ደመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ዘተክዕወ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ እምደመ ፡ አቤል፤ ጸድቅ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደመ ፡ ዘካርያስ፡ ፡ ወልደ ፤ በራክዮ ፡ ዘቀተልዎ ፡ በማእከለ ፡፤ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ። ወጳውሎስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፤ በተአምኖ ፡ ሐየሰ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ አቤል ፡ እምዘ ፡ ቃየል፤ ፡ ዘአብኦ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወበእንቲአሁ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መሥዋዕት ፡ ዘሦዓ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፤ ወመዊቶሂ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ ተናገረ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ማኅቶት፤፤ ፡ ወመርሕ ፡ ለሐዳስ ፡ ሥርዓት ፡ ወንዝኃተ ፡ ደም ፡ ነባቢ ፡ ዘይጌይስ ፡ ተናግሮ ፡ እምዘ ፡ አቤል ።
Enoch also said: "I heard the blood of Abel cry out and accuse his brother." Our fathers also, the sons of Adam, swore by the blood of Abel before the water of the flood. Our Lord also said to the scribes and the Pharisees: Which of the prophets did your fathers not kill? "So that there may come upon you the blood of all the righteous that has been shed on the earth, from the blood of Abel the righteous to the blood of Zacharias, son of Barachias, whom they killed in the middle of the Temple." Paul also said: "It is by faith that the sacrifice of Abel was better than that of Cain, (a sacrifice) which he had offered to God, and it is thanks to it that he had the testimony that he was righteous, the sacrifice which he had sacrificed to God. It is thanks to it that, though dead, he still speaks." He says again: "Jesus, lamp and guide for a new covenant and sprinkling of speaking blood that speaks better than that of Abel."
ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኃጥኦ ፡ አዳም ፡ ለአቤል ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለቃየል ፡ አይቴ ፡ ሀሎ ፣ እከ ፡ ወሜጠ ፡ ሉቱ ፡ ቃለ ፡ መዓት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በምንት ፡ ኣአምር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ፣ እየ ፡ ወሮጸ ፡ አዳም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ግዱፈ ፡ ለአቤል ፡ ወሐቀፈ” ፡ ክሳዶ ፡ ለአቤል ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መኑ ፡ ቀተለከ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ በድን ፡ ቃየል ፡ ቀተለኒ ። ወበከየ ፡ አዳም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ በሞተ ፡ አቤል ፡ '፪ሱባዔ ፣ ዓመታት ። ወአመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አቤል፤ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኅበ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ብስራትከ ፡ ኦአዳም ፡ አበ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወልድከሰ ፡ አቤል ፡ ተሳለመ ፡ ዕፀዊሃ ፡ ለገነት ፡ ወዓርገት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ኀበ ፣
Then, when Adam did not find Abel his son, he said to Cain: "Where is your brother?" (Cain) returned a word of anger to him, saying: "How should I know where my brother is?" Adam ran to the valley, found Abel lying there, and embraced the neck of Abel. He said to him: "Who killed you?" The corpse said to him: "Cain killed me." Adam mourned the death of Abel with his wife for four weeks of years. When Abel died, angels came to Adam and said to him: "(Behold?) good news for you, Adam, father of the world: your

እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ መሥዋዕት ፡ ውክፍት ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀደመከ ፡ 'መዊተ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ቀደመከ ፡ በዊአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አዳም ፡ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ" ፣፡ ተናዘዘ ፡ ወቀበሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓተ ፡ መዛግብት ። በረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
son Abel has greeted (?) the trees of paradise and his soul has ascended to God as an accepted sacrifice; just as he died before you, so he has entered before you into the holy place." When Adam heard these words, he was comforted and buried him (Abel) in the cave of treasures. May his blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]

On this day the holy Callinicus the bishop became a martyr. This (took place) thus: When the report reached the emperor Diocletian the impious that the saint was teaching people to despise his gods, he sent messengers to apprehend and torture him. When the saint learned of the arrival of the emperor's messengers, he gathered the people in the city of Aousim, offered the sacrifice, and gave them the holy mysteries, the flesh and blood of our Lord Christ. Then he said to them: "From now on, you will no longer see my face." All shed bitter tears, but they could not turn him from his will. Then he went out and surrendered himself to the emperor's messengers. (They) took him and delivered him to the governor of the city of Antinoë so that he might (have him) tortured, and he subjected him to great torture. God comforted him and made him constant. Then, (the governor) took him with him to the city of Edkou; he subjected him to great torture there. When he was weary of torturing him, he ordered his head to be cut off and to be split down to the shoulders. Then they split his head down to his shoulders and (the governor) took him with him to bring him to the city of Toukh. As they were traveling on the river, the saint knew that his death was near; he gave an order to a believer among the sailors and said to him: "When we have arrived at the port and I am dead, throw my body on the hill." When he had said this, he died. They arrived at the port, the man took the body of the saint and threw it on the hill. And behold, believers, to whom an angel of God had ordered (it), came, took the body of the saint, buried it, and hid it at their place until the end of the days of persecution. A church was built for him, his body was placed there, and great miracles and wonders occurred from it. May his prayer and his blessing be with us.
ወመንክራት ፡ ዓበይት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፣ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቴዎናስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፤ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡፤ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፲ወ፪ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ማእምረ ፡ ወጠቢበ ፡ ወጽኑዓ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወኮነ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ምግባራቲሁ ፡ ወያፈቅርዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወበእንተ ፡ አእምሮቱ ፡ ወጥበቡ ፤ ሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድ ስት ፡ ድንግል ፤ ማርያም ። እስመ ፣፤ መሃይምናን ፣ ኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ይጹልዩ ፡
On this day also died the holy father Abba Theonas, patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarchs, he is the sixteenth. This saint was learned, wise, and firm in the right faith; he was good in all his actions and everyone loved him. Because of his knowledge and his wisdom, he built a church in the city of Alexandria in the name of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, for the believers could not pray and

ወይቄድሱ ፡ ዘእንበለ' ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓታት ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ በእንተ ፡ 'ፍርሃት ፡ እምከሓዲያን ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ የሐይጦሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲ ያነ ፣ ሠናይተ ፤ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ወአጥመቆሙ‹ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወበቀዳሚት ፡ ዓመት ፤ እምሚመቱ ፡ አጥመቆ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ዘኮነ ፣፤ ሊቀ ፡፤ ጳጳሳተ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ። ወበ፳ዓመት ፤ እምሚመቱ ፡፤ ሜሞ ፡፤ አናጐንጢሰ ። ወበ፲ወ፪ዓመት ፤ እምሚመቱ ፡ ሜሞ ፡ ዲያቆነ፤ ። 'ወበ፲ ወ፪ዓመት ፡ እምሚመቱ ፡ ጫሞ ፡ ቀሲሰ። ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ቴዎናስ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ "በውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡እስክንድርያ ፡ ስልብዮስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ዘኢየአምን ፡ ከመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡፤ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ህላዌ ። ወአውገዞ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፣ ወአብጠለ ፤ ሃይማኖቶ ፡ እኪተ ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ 'ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ካዕበ” ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ቆዝሞስ ፤ ወዲሚያኖስ ፤ ወአኃዊሆሙ ፡ ወእሞሙ ። ወሶበ ፡፣ ፈጸመ”" ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ሑረቶ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ወተፈጸመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በሚመቱ?! ፡ ፳ወቿዓመት”፤ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ፣
celebrate the Eucharist, except in the caves, in secret, for fear of the pagans. This saint seduced them (= the pagans) so well that he built a beautiful church, converted many of them, and baptized them with Christian baptism. In the first year of his tenure, he baptized the saint Peter who was patriarch after him. In the fifth year of his tenure, he ordained him reader; in the twelfth year of his tenure, he consecrated him deacon; in the sixteenth year of his tenure, he consecrated him priest. In the time of this saint Abba Theonas, Sabellius the impious appeared in the city of Alexandria, who did not believe that the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit (were) one nature (hellawë). This father excommunicated him and destroyed his evil faith. In the time of this father also, the saints Cosmas, Damian, their brothers, and their mother were martyrs. When this father had finished his beautiful career, had pleased God, and had completed twenty-eight (?) years in his tenure, he died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ቅዳሴሃ ፣ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ በደብረ ፡ አባ ፡ ሲኖዳ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ ወትንብልናሃ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፊላታዎስ ፡ ፳፻ ወ፪፻ ነፍስ ፡ ወሳቤላ፡ ወለተ ፡፤ ሕርቃላስ ፡ ፈካሪተ ፤ ሕልም ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
On this day also (is) the consecration of the church of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary in the monastery of Abba Shenoute. May her (= Mary's) prayer, her blessing, and her intercession be with us, amen. [On this day also, nine thousand seven hundred souls were martyrs with Philotheus, and Sibyl, the daughter of Hercules, the interpreter of dreams. May their prayer and their blessing be with our king Iyasu for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 3RD OF TERR
አመ ፡ ፻ለጥር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡፣ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፤ ተቀትሉ ፡፣ ሕፃናት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ንጹሓን ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፤ ፲፻፻ ወ ፪፻፻ ወ ግ፻ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ ንጉሥ፤ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጸንሖሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ሰገል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ 'ወሶበ ፡ ኢተመይጡ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ወፈነወ ፡ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለሕፃናት ፡ ዘቤተ ፡ ልሔም ፡ ዘይሁዳ ፡ ወዘደወላ ፡ እንዘ ፡ መዋዕሊሆሙ ፡ ፪ዓመት ፡ ወዘይንእስ ፡ በከመ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ዘተጠየቀ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሰገል ። ወይቤ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሐሩ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሰገል ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ጸመልአከ፣፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ በሕልም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወንሣእ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the one hundred and forty-four thousand holy, innocent, righteous, and martyr children were killed. This (took place) thus: when Herod, the impious king, waited for the magi to return to him and when they did not return, he was greatly angered, sent (soldiers), and (had) the children of Bethlehem of Judah and its region killed whose age (was) two years and under, according to the age he had learned from the magi. The holy Gospel says: When the magi had departed, an angel of God appeared to Joseph in a dream saying: "Arise, take

ወለእሙ ፡ ወሖር ፡!'ኀበ ፡ ምድረ” ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወንበር ፡ ሀየ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ እብለከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ የኃሥሦ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ወነሥኦ ፤፡ ለሕፃን ፤ ወለእሙ ፡ በሌሊት ፤ ወመጽአ!" ፡ 'ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ፤! ፡ ከመ ፣ ይትፈጸም፤" ፡ ቃለ ፡ ነቢይ ፤ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እምግብጽ ፡ጸዋዕክዎ ፡ ለወልድየ ። ወሄሮድስሰ፣ ርጉም ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ በሕሊናሁ ፣ በቀቲሎቶሙ ፡ ለሕፃናት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፤ እምፕልቆሙ ። እስመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ገብረ" ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ባሕቲታ ። ጐየ ፡ እግዚ እነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ እምቅድመ!፤! ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ በኪነ ፡ ጥበቡ ። እስመ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ 'ወቀተሎ ፡ ወኢይመውት?፤" ፡ በከመ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ለኃይለ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ የሐልዩ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ምትሀት ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ጐየ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ ትንቢት" ፡ ወይምሀረነ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንጉየይ ፡ እምእ ኩይ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ያጥፍኦሙ ፡ ለጣዖታተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወእፎ ፡ ተክህሎ ፡ ለሄሮድስ ፡ ቀቲሎቶሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ እለ፤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተመክነየ ፡ በምክንያት ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ መልእክቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ቄሣር ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጐልቀኑዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡፣ ሕፃናት ፡ ንኡሳን ፡ እንዘ ፣ መዋፅሊሆሙ ፡ ፪ዓመት ፡ ወዘይንእስ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብዎሙ፣ ንዋየ ፡ ወአልባሳተ ፡ 'ወይትጐለቁ፤ ፡ እምሰራዊቱ፤ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ብዙ ኃን ፡ ሕፃናት ፣፤ ምስለ ፡ እሞሙ?" ፡ ወአውዕፅአ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፤ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ፻፻ሐራ ፡ ወሐረድዎሙ” ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ አድባር ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይቤ ፤ ቃል ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ በራማ ፡ ብካይ ፡ ወላሕ ፡ ራሔል ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ደቂቃ ፤ ወኢፈቀደት ፡ ትትናዘዝ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘተሐጥኡ ፡ ደቂቃ ። እስመ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ልሔም ፡ ትሠመይ ፡ በራሔል ። ወይቤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ በአቡ ፡ ቀለም ሲስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሕፃናት ፣ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይጸርሑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ እስከ ፡ ማእዜኑ ፡ ትትቤቀል?ቶ" ፡ ለነ ፡ እምዘ፤ ፡ ዓመፀነ ። ይቤ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወርኢኩቶ፥። ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ተውህበ ፡ ለለ 88እምኔሆሙ ፡ አልባስ ፡ ጸዓዳ ፤ ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ አዕርፉ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይትፈጸሙ ፡ አኃዊክሙ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወይቤ ፡ ካዕ በ ፡ እስመ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘይሴብሑ ፡ ባቲ ፡ 8እንስሳ ፡ ወካህናተ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ኢያአምራ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፲፻፻ወ 8፪፻፻ወጣ፻ ፡ እሉ ፡ ሕፃናት ፤ ንጹሓን ፡ ዘኢያርኩሱ ፤ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ በፍትወት ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ። ብፁዓን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወብፁዓት ፡ ከርሣት ፡ እለ ፡ ጾርዎሙ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀረነ ፡ በትንብልናሆሙ ፡ ወስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዓለመ ፡
the child and his mother, go into the land of Egypt and stay there until I tell you; for Herod seeks the child to kill him." Joseph arose, took the child and his mother, by night, and came into the land of Egypt so that the word of the prophet might be fulfilled which says: Out of Egypt I have called my son. As for Herod the cursed, he wanted in his mind to kill the children to kill our Lord Christ - glory to him! - in their number, for our Lord Christ performed all the actions of a man, with the sole exception of sin. Our Lord Christ fled from before Herod by a ruse of his wisdom. Indeed, if Herod had found him and killed him, he would not have died as befits the power of his divinity, so that one would have thought that his incarnation (was) an illusion. Because of this, our Lord fled into the land of Egypt so that the prophecy might be fulfilled, (to) teach us also to flee from evil and to destroy the idols of the land of Egypt. How was it possible for Herod to kill these many small children? He imagined a ruse and dispatched (messengers) to all the cities, (making them) say to them (= the inhabitants): "A letter from the emperor Caesar has arrived for me, and he orders to count all the small children whose age (is) two years and under so that they may be given money and clothes and be counted among his soldiers." Because of this, many small children were gathered with their mothers, and Herod had ten thousand soldiers depart from him who massacred them (= the children) on the mountains in a single day; the prophecy of the prophet was fulfilled which says: A voice has been heard in Rama, weeping and lamentations, Rachel weeping for her children; she does not want to be comforted because her children are no more. Indeed, Bethlehem is named after Rachel. Saint John the evangelist says in the Apocalypse that he himself saw the souls of these holy children; these were crying out, saying: "How long (will you delay) to avenge us on those who have treated us unjustly?" Saint John says: "I saw, and behold, a white robe was given to each of them and they were told: 'Rest now until your brother martyrs who (are) in the world are complete.'" He also says: "The praise with which the four Living Creatures and the Priests of heaven praised him, no one knows except the one hundred and forty-four thousand, those pure children who have not defiled their bodies with lust. They (are) with God at all times. Happy are they and happy the wombs that bore them! May God have mercy on us through their intercession; glory to God for ever and ever, amen!
ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ 'አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ፥ ፡ መጣዕ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ ንግሥት ። እስመ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ብዑላን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወበብሩር ። ወለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ አዕተዉ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ጸውዖ፥" ፡ መልአከ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ወአውዕኦ ፡ እም ቤተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ። ወበህየ ፡ መንኩሰ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወአውልአ ፡ ማየ ፡ፈውስ ፡እምኩኩሕ ፡ ወገ ብረ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ" ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ወተዝካረ ፡ አባ ፡ አሞን ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፀፍር ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ ርእሶን ፡ ለአንስት ፡ ወኢክህለ ፡ ያስጥሞ ፡ ማዕበለ ፡ ኃጢአት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
On this day also died the holy father Abba Mata'. [The name of the father of this saint was Abraham and the name of his mother, Negest. They were very rich in gold and silver. As for this saint, the day they brought him a wife, the angel of God, the holy Gabriel, called him, brought him out of his father's house, and brought him to the house of Abba Pachomius. He became a monk there, then went into the land of Ethiopia and remained to conduct a (spiritual) combat for many days. He made healing water gush from a rock, performed many miracles and wonders, and died in peace. And (there is) the commemoration of Abba Amon who used to dress women's hair and whom the flood of sin could not submerge.] May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 26] {#po.45.p26}

### READING FOR THE 4TH OF TERR
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፪ለጥር ፡ ምንባብ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፍልሰቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ውፅአ ፡ ክፍሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር፤ ፡!'ኅበ ፡ ብሔረ" ፡ እስያ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ያአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡እኩያን ፡ ዓላዊያን ፡ 'ወግዙፋን ፡ ክሳዶሙጎ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ እምእግዚእነ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ወኑዛዜ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክፍሉ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ባርኮሮስ ፡ ረድኡ ። ወተፅዕኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወይፈቅዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሐሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ ኤፌሶን ፡፤ ወተሰብረ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወተሰቅሉ ፡ ለለ፳8እምኔሆሙ ፡ 'በጽላት ፡ እምፅፀወ?" ፡ ሐመር ። ወለረድኡሰ ፡ ባርኮሮስ ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ መዋግደ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ኅበ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ደሴት ፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ 'እስመ ፡ ውእቱ?" ፡ ነበረ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ መዋግደ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ጓመዓልተ ፡ ወጣሌሊተ ፡እስከ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወወገሮ'! ፡ ባ ሕር፣ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘነበረ ፤ ውስቴቱ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ባርኮሮስ፤ ፡ ወአእኩትዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር?፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡፤ ተራከቦቶሙ ። ወተንሥኡ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኤፌሶን ፡ ወኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስብኩ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ ዓላዊያን! ። ወተመክነዩ ፡ በምክንያት፤፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ያነድድ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምንዱደ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ዘለብእሲት ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ሮምና ፤ ወባርኮሮስ፤ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ኮነ፤ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ። ወኮነት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day (is) the death of the holy John, the apostle evangelist. When the lot fell to him to go to the land of Asia, this saint was afflicted and wept, for he knew that they (= the inhabitants) were wicked, impious, and (that) their neck (was) stiff. Then he received power and comfort from our Lord and departed toward (what was) his lot; with him (was) Prochorus, his disciple. [They embarked on a boat, they wanted to go to the city of Ephesus. The ship was wrecked and each of them (= the passengers) clung to planks of the ship's frame. The waves of the sea brought his (= John's) disciple Prochorus to an island. As for the saint John, he remained in the midst of the waves of the sea for forty days and forty nights until God gave an order, and the sea threw him into the place where his disciple Prochorus was; they gave thanks to God for their reunion. They arose and entered the city of Ephesus. It was not possible for them to preach in the name of our Lord Jesus Christ, for these people (were) wicked (and) impious. They used a ruse: John would light the fire in the boiler room of a bathhouse that (belonged) to a woman by the name of Romana, and Prochorus, his disciple, became the bath


[p. 28] {#po.45.p28}
ታመነድቦሙ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወተኃሥሮሙ ፡ 'ኅሥረተ ፡ ዓቢየ፤ ፡ ወትዘብጦሙ!፡ ወትረግሞሙ ። ወበእንተ ፤ ፍልሰቶሙ' ፡ ወንዴቶሙ"፤ ፡ ወረሰየቶሙ ፡ ላቲ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ ወጸሐፈት ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ጦማረ ፡ ሜጦሙ ። ወነበሩ ፡ በኅቤሃ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኃሣር ፡ ዓቢይ ፤ እስከ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ 'ከመ፣ ይትሐፀብ። ። "ወኮነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብለኔ፥ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ እምጊዜ ፡ ሐነጽዋ ፡ ወሐነቀቶ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአይዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡፤ ዘሞተ ፤ ወበጽሐ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ዮሐንስ ፡ "ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ይርአይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየቶ ፡ "ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ሮምና ፡ ረገመቶ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ መጻእከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሳለቅ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ወትትፈሣሕ ፡፤ በሞቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግዚእየ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ በየውሀቱ ፡፣ ወቅድስናሁ፤' ፡ ይቤላ ፤ ኢት'ፍርሂ ፣ ወኢትሕዝኒ ። ወቀርበ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምውት ፡ ወዓተበ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፤ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ማሕየዊ ፡ ወነፍሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ወሐይወ” ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወከልሑ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ከመ፣ ይስግዱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ርምና ፡ 'እግዝእቱ ፡ እስመ" ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ወትስእል፤ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድግ ፡ ላቲ ፤ ኩሎ ፡ አበሳሃ ፡ ዘገብ
boy. This woman subjected them to great torments and overwhelmed them with many vexations; she beat them and insulted them. Because of their status as strangers and their poverty, she made them her slaves and wrote a (false) deed of sale regarding them. They remained with her in (this) very humiliating (situation) until the son of the governor of the city came into the bathhouse to wash. There had been a satanic power in this bathhouse since the moment it had been built; it strangled the young son of the governor, and he died immediately. When the people of the city gathered to see the adolescent who was dead, the saint John came with them and stood watching like all the people. When this woman, Romana, saw him, she insulted him and said to him: "You have come to mock me and rejoice at the death of my master's son." But the saint said to her in his gentleness and holiness: "Do not be afraid and do not be afflicted," and he approached the dead, made the sign of the life-giving cross over him, and breathed into his face; immediately, (the adolescent) returned to life. All the people of the city cried out and came to prostrate themselves before the saint John, in particular Romana his mistress. She was weeping and begging him to forgive her for all the faults she had committed against him.
ረት ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወዲዮስቆሪዶስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ደንገፀ ፡ ወሞተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነገርዎ ፤ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፤ አተበ ፣ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፤ ወአንሥኦ ፡ እሙታን ፡ ወአምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ "ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፥" ፡ ወተጠምቀ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሰብአ ፡ ኤፌሶ ንሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዘለፎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አምልኮቶሙ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ አኃዞሙ ፡ መዓት ፡እስከ ፡ ወገርዎ ፡ በአእባን ፡'ወተመይጡ ፡ አእባን ፡ ወሰበሩ ፡ ጣዖታቲሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ "ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ድምፀ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወድልቅልቅ፣ ወሞቱ ፡፤ ፻፻ዕደው ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ወአንሥኦሙ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ?፤ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወተጠምቁ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወበጽሐት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፤ ብእሲት ፡፣ ዘአኃዞ ፣፡ ለወልዳ" ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ ። ወሶበ ፤ ሰአለቶ ፡ አኃዘ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዘየማን ፡ ወዓተበ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ድን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሰብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ። ወካልእ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘስ ኩብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓራት ፤ እምአመ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነጸሮ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ጸርሐ ፤ በቃል ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ መሐረኒ ፡ ሐዋርያሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት" ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አሚኖቶ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፤ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወአምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ።
[When Dioscorides learned that his son was dead, he was seized with trembling and died. When this was told to the saint John, he made the sign of the cross (over him) and raised him from the dead, and (Dioscorides) believed in our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! He was baptized with all the people of his house in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit. As for the people of Ephesus, when the saint John reprimanded them because of their worship of idols, anger seized them to the point that they threw stones at him, but these returned (backwards) and broke their idols. When the saint John prayed to his God, there was a great crash and an earthquake, and three hundred men died. Then the saint John prayed and raised them; all the people of the city believed and were baptized in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit. A woman whose son had been possessed by an unclean spirit came to him (= John). When she begged him, he took his (= the son's) right hand and made the sign of the cross. (The son) rose up healed and glorified God the Most High. Another man who had been lying on a bed for twelve years, when he saw the saint John, cried out with a loud voice, saying: "Have pity on me, apostle of our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him!" When (John) saw his faith, he said to him: "In the name of our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! - rise up." (The man) rose up quickly and believed in our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him!


[p. 30] {#po.45.p30}
ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ መልአ ፡ ትምይንተ ፡ ወቅንዓተ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበኪ ። ወረከብዎ ፡ ፪ዕደው ፡፣ እለ ፡ የኃልፉ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ምንት ፡ ያበክየከ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሀበኒ ፡፤ ፪'ፅደወ ፡ መሠርያነ" ፡ ዘስሞሙ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወአብሮኮሮስ ፡ ለእመ ፡ አኃዝክምዎሙቶ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አነ ፡ እሁበከ ፡ ወርቀ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አኃዝዎሙ ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ይቅትልዎሙ ፡ በጽሚት ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዲዮስቆሪዶስ ፡ አድኃኖሙ ።
As for Satan, when he saw all these miracles, he was filled with perfidy and jealousy: he transformed himself into an officer of the emperor and entered the city weeping. Two men who were passing by met him and said to him: "What makes you weep?" He said to them: "The emperor has given me two magician men whose names (are) John and Prochorus; if you attack them for me, I will give you gold." Immediately, they apprehended them and wanted to kill them secretly. When Dioscorides learned (this), he delivered them.]
ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ክርስትን ፡ ወገብረ፥ ፡ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ገነውተ ፡ ጣዖታት፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኢአምኑ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሰይጣን ፣ የሀውኮሙ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ አላ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ "ውእቱ ፡ ዘየዓቅቦሙ ፡ ለኅሩያኒሁ ። ወጸማ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወምንዳቤ ፡ በጽሖ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሜጦሙ ፡ ለዙሎሙዮ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግዝፍና ፣ ልቦሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኤፌሶን ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ብዝኃ ፡ አፍቅሮቶሙ፥" ፡ 'ለአምልኮ ፡ ጣዖ ታት፤፥ ። ወበጸማ ፤ ዓቢይ ፤ አግብኦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስ
Many people believed in our Lord Christ - glory to him! -, (John) baptized them with Christian baptism and performed many miracles and wonders before them. All the people of the city believed in our Lord Christ except the priests of the idols; these did not believe and wanted to kill the saint John. Satan incited them against him so that they would kill him, but God protects his elect. Great pains and afflictions came to the saint John until he had converted them all to the knowledge of God. The holy apostle Paul was a witness to the stubbornness of the people of the city of Ephesus and the greatness of their love for idol worship. With great difficulty, (John) brought them all to faith in Christ and instituted bishops and priests for them.
ቶስ ፡ ወሜመ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆጳሳተ ፡ ወካህናተ፤ ። ወወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አህጉር ፡ እለ ፡ የአውድዋ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ እስያ ፡ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ወአግብኦሙ፡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሐይወ ፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ጓዓመተ ፡ ወልህቀ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኢጥዕመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ድንግልናሁ ፡ ወንጽሕናሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ዕሩያነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ ወንጌሎ ፡ ወአቡቀለምሲሰ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደሴተ ፡ በትሞስ ፡ እንተ ፣ ምልእት ፣ እምሥጢራት ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፣፤ ይጾመልእክታተ ፡ ዘበመጽሐፈ" ፡ ሐዋርያ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ዘረፈቀ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እንግድዓሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፤ በጊዜ ፡ ድራር ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፣ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘያገብአከ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ስቅለቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ 'ድንግል ፤ ማርያም”" ። ወይቤላ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለድንግል ፡ ናሁ ፡ ወልድኪ ፡ ወይቤሎ"! ፡ ነያ ፡ እምከ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ዝኬ ፡ እፎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እመኬ ፡ ፈቀድኩ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ እመጽእ ፡ ሚላዕሌከ ።
He departed toward the cities that surround the whole land of Asia, converted them all (= the inhabitants), and brought them to the knowledge of God. The saint John lived ninety years and became very old. He did not suffer death by the sword like all the apostles because of his virginity and his purity, so that all might be equal in glory. He wrote his Gospel and the Apocalypse, (a vision) which he saw on the island of Patmos, which (is) full of (divine) mysteries, and he wrote the three epistles which (are) in the book of the apostle. It is he who rested on the breast of our Lord at the moment of the Supper and said to him: "My Lord, who is the one who will betray you?" It is he who stood there at the moment of the crucifixion of our Lord with the Virgin Mary. Our Lord said to the Virgin: "Behold your son." And he said to him (= to John): "Behold your mother." It is about him that Peter said: "My Lord, what about him?" Our Lord said to him: "If I want him to remain until I come, what is that to you?"
ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከመ ፣ ፍልሰቱ ፡፤ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ 'ለሕዝብ ፤ ወወሀቦሙ”" ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሞ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽንዑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትእት ፡ ወናዘዞሙ ፡ ወባረኮሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡
When the saint John knew that his departure from this world was near, he called the people, gave them the body and blood of Christ, prescribed for them to be firm in the right faith, comforted them, and blessed them. Then he ordered his


[p. 32] {#po.45.p32}
አዘዞ ፤ ለረድኡ ፡ ባርኮሮስ" ፣ ወለ፪ካልኣን ፡ እምነ ፡ አኃው ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥኡ ፡ ም ስሌሆሙ ፡ 'መክሪተ ፡ ወማኅፄ ፡ ወይትልውዎ?'" ። ወወፅአ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ኤፌሶን ፡ በንስቲት ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክርዩ ፡ ዓዘቅተ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወተዓርቀ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ወተረፈ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፳ ቀሚስ ፡ ዘሰንዶናት ። ወአንሥአ ፡ እደ ዊሁ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ወአስተፋነዎሙ'?" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወይንግርዎሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽንዑ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ ወይግበሩ ፡ ምግባረተ ፡ ሠናያተ ፤ እስመ ፤ ሀለዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈድዮሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡፣ ለለ8፳በከመ ፡ ምግባሩ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አነ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ እምደምክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፤ ኢኃደጉ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምትእዛዛተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ" ፡ ዘነገርኩ ክሙ ፥ ኪያሁ ። ወአንትሙሰ ፡ እምይእዜ ፤ ኢትሬእዩኒ ፡ ገጽየ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ዘንተ ፡ በከዩ ፡ ወተአምጐ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ወኀደግዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓዘቅት ፡ ወሖሩ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይሰግድ ፡ በብረኪሁ ፡ ወይጹሊ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ ሕዝብ ፤ ዘንተ ፡ ነደ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ወሐዘኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወኢረከቡ ፡ መቃብሮ ፡ 'አላ ፡ ረከቡ ፣ አሣእኒሁ ፡ ወልብሶ ፡ ሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአን ከሩ ፡ እምግብሩ ፡ ዘአዕረፈ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ዕረፍት" ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡
disciple Prochorus and two others of the brothers (= the faithful) to take a shovel and a pickaxe with them and to follow him. He went some distance outside the city of Ephesus and ordered them to dig a pit; he descended into it, stripped off his clothes, and there remained on him only a tunic of linen fabric. He raised his hands and prayed, he dismissed them (= the disciples) so that they might return to the city and tell the brothers to be firm in the faith of Christ and to do good works, for it would return to all that God would reward them each according to his works. He said to them again: "As for me, I am innocent of your blood, for I have not left any of the prescriptions of God without making it known to you. As for you, from now on, you will no longer see my face." When he had said this, they wept, kissed his hands and his feet, left him in the pit, and went away. The saint was prostrated on his knees and praying. When the people learned of this, their hearts were inflamed and they were greatly afflicted: they all went out, did not find his tomb, but found his shoes and his garment. They glorified God and admired the acts of his disciple John who had died this death. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us, amen.
ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለጊዮርጊስ ፡ ወማቲና፤ ፡ ሊቀ”። ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወታኦድራ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወአባ ፡ ሊቃኖስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ናርዶስ ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ሊባኖስ ፡ ዘቀ ተለ ፤ ከይሴ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ይዝብጦ ፡ በበትር ፡ ወይጹሊ ፡ እስከ ፡ የሐ ብጥ ፡ እገሪሁ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also (is) the commemoration of George, of Matina the patriarch, of Theodora the martyr, of Abba Liganos, of Abba Nardos of Dabra Libanos who killed a serpent by his prayer without striking it with a stick and (who) prayed until his feet swelled. May God, through his prayer, have mercy on our king Iyasu for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 5TH OF TERR
አመ ፡ ፳ለጥር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ አውሳግንዮስ፤ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ያመልኮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወብዙኅ ፡ ምሕረቱ ፡ ወምጽዋቱ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወምስኪናን ። ወሶበ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ አምሳለ ፣ መስቀል ፡ ከዋክብት ፡ በሰማይ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ የአምን ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ያአምር ፡ ዛተ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ወቀርበ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አውሳግንዮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዛቲ ፤ ትእምርቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። እስመ ፡ ኢሀሎ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ዘይደፍር ፡ ወይዘክር ፡ ስሞ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞኦሙ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ፤ ፡ ለጸላእቱ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the saint Eugenius became a martyr. This saint was a soldier in the time of Constantine; he worshipped God and great (were) his mercy and his alms to the poor and the needy. When the stars in the sky appeared to Constantine in the image of the Cross, before he believed in our Lord Christ - and he did not know this sign -, this saint Eugenius approached him and said to him: "This (is) the sign of our Lord Christ"; indeed, there was no one in the army of Constantine who dared to mention the name of Christ except this saint. When Constantine had defeated his enemies thanks to the sign of the glorious


[p. 34] {#po.45.p34}
መስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዌ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ ወአቆመ ፡ ቀርነ ፡ 'ሃይማኖተ ፣ ክርስቶስ፤፤ ። ወሐይወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሐራ ፤ አውሳግንዮስ፤ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፤ ውሉዱ?!" ፡ እስከ ፤ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለሉልያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ፻ወ፲ ዓመት ።
Cross, he became a true Christian and raised the power of the faith of Christ. This soldier, Eugenius, lived after Constantine and after his son until the time of Julian the impious; at that time, his age was one hundred and ten years.
ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ መራኅብተ ፡ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ረከቦሙ ፡ ለ፪፤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትባአሱ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወፈቀዶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር፤፤ ፡ ፍትሐ፤ ፤ ማእከሌሆሙ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ክቡር!" ። 'ወሶበ ፡ ገብረ፤ ፣ ማእከሌሆሙ ፤ ፍትሐ! ፡ ወሰላመ ፡ ሖረ" ፣ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣፤ ሉልያኖስ ፡ ወአስተዋደዮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አውሳግንዮስ፡፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ወመስፍነ ፡ በፈቃዱ ። ወአብጽሖ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወገሠጾ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘጫመከ ፡ 'መኩንነ ፡ ወመስፍነ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ አውሳግንዮስ ፡ ወለምንት ፡ አንተ ፡ ኃደገ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ዘነፍስከ ፡ በእዴሁ ፤ ወሰገድከ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ርኩሳት ፤ ወኢትተልዎሙ ፡ ለነገሥት ፡እለ ፡ኮኑ፣ እምቅድሜከ ። ወአንሰ ፡ ነበርኩ፤ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ዘከማከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ፳ዓመተ?፤" ፡ ወምስለ ፡ 'ውሉዱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ 'ወኢኮነ ፣ እምውስቴቶሙ ፡ ዘየአኪ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ኀደገ ፡ 'ፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ወሰገድከ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ርኩሳት ። 'ተምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ላዕሌሁ፤ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስቅ ልዎ ፡ ወይረስዩ። ፡ መኃትወ ፡ እሳት፥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገበዋቲሁ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይትዔገሥ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ። ወሶበ ፣ መጽኡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ሰአሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትዔገሠ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡እስከ ፡ ይጹሊ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ቅድስተ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳንቶ ፡ 'በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት?" ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡
While he was walking one day in the streets of the city of Antioch, he saw two (men) arguing with each other. He wanted to render a judgment between them, for he was an honored old man. When he had rendered his judgment and (made) peace between them, a man went to the emperor Julian, denounced this saint, and said to him (= to the emperor): "Behold, Eugenius has become governor and judge on his own authority." The emperor had the saint brought, reprimanded him, and said to him: "Who instituted you governor and judge?" The saint Eugenius said to him: "Why have you abandoned the worship of the God of heaven in whose hand (is) your soul? And (why) have you prostrated yourself before impure idols and do not follow the emperors who preceded you? As for me, I remained a soldier, like you, with Constantine for eighty years and with his sons also, and there was none among them who was worse than you, for you have abandoned the fear of God and have prostrated yourself before impure idols." The emperor was greatly angered against him, he ordered him to be crucified and to place flaming torches against his sides; they did all this to him, and he remained constant for the name of our Lord Christ. When (the emperor) was weary of torturing him, he ordered his head to be cut off with the sword. When (the soldiers) came to cut off his head, he asked them to wait until he had prayed. When he had finished his prayer, they cut off his holy head and he received the crown of martyrdom with all the saints in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእለ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፳ወ፳ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ አባ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡፤ እስመ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ እምነገደ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ምእመናን ፡ በእግዚ እነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወፍጹማን ፡ በምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ስመ፥ ፡ አቡሁ ፡፤ ስምዖን ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ በርባራ0 ። ወወሀቦሙ ፡፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡፪ተ፣፤ ዘርአ ፡ ለዘይልህቅ፤ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወለዘይንእስ ፤ ስሙ።= ፡ ማቴዎስ ። ወለዝንቱ ፡ አባ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በከርሥ ፡ ረከባ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አቡሬስ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ ዓድጉ ፡ ወሰዓመ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡
[On this day also died Abba Matthew, patriarch of Alexandria; among the patriarchs, he is the twenty-eighth. As for this Abba Matthew, his parents were of the race of Israel, believing in our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! - and accomplished in beautiful actions. The name of his father (was) Simon and the name of his mother, Barbara. God gave them two descendants, the name of the elder (was) Jacob and the name of the younger, Matthew. As for this Abba Matthew, while he was in the womb (of his mother), Abba Peter, bishop of the city of Abures, met his mother; when he had descended from his donkey, he kissed her (= the woman's) womb and said to her: "Blessed (are you), woman, and blessed (is) the fruit of your womb; behold, there will come

ዘእንበለ ፣ በቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ለጊዜ ፤ ጸሎት ። ወተወክፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ገድለ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፪ኣመት ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ትዕግሥቶ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቡራኬ ፥ ወፍትሐተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እሁ ፡ያዕቆብ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ገድለ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሞኦ ፡ ለሰይጣን ።
from you a luminous star that will shine in its glory like Mark the evangelist." When Matthew was fifteen years old, his parents died. After that, he went to Abba Abraham, superior of the monastery of the saint Epiphane, and asked him to clothe him in the monastic habit. (The superior) said to him: "Come to an agreement with your brother and return." Having heard (this), Abba Matthew returned with an afflicted heart; on the way, the angel Michael met him in the guise of a monk and told him that he would become patriarch and that miracles and wonders would occur through his hands. Arrived at his brother Jacob, he urged him to allow him to become a monk. As his brother refused him, he went to the monastery of Abba Amoni in the mountain of Tounah and took refuge in the church. When the monks prayed for his brother, (the latter) authorized him (to become a monk) and he went to the monastery of the saint Abunafa, and Abba Abraham made him a monk. He fought (spiritually) in fasting and in prayer until he surpassed the monks. After a year had passed for him, Abba Peter went to Abba Abraham. Seeing the constancy of Abba Matthew and his wisdom, he asked Abraham to give him to him so that he might be his disciple. The bishop took him with him against his will and (Matthew) remained with him, keeping vigil all night from sunset until the sun rose. When he was eighteen years old, the bishop took him and ordained him priest against his will. When Satan saw his (diligence in) vigil, he entered the heart of a pretty woman and (did) so well that she appeared to him (= to Matthew) and solicited him for an evil desire. Seeing (her), Abba Matthew prayed to God with tears to the point of wanting to die. Then he went to the bishop and begged him to send him back to his monastery. As the bishop refused him, he went into his storeroom, took a priestly garment of the bishop of great price, cut it into small pieces with a razor, and put it back where it was before. The next day, the bishop said to Abba Matthew: "Bring me a priestly garment so that I may officiate today." When (Matthew) brought it before him, it fell like wood shavings. Seeing (this), the bishop was greatly angered and said: "Who did this?" Abba Matthew said: "It is I who (did) it; if you forbid me to leave, I will do (a misdeed) greater than this again rather than fall into lust." The bishop in anger sent him back and wrote a (letter of) excommunication regarding him to the people of the monastery so that he would prostrate himself two hundred times night and day and would not remain in a cell but in the church at the time of prayer. Abba Matthew sustained this (spiritual) combat for two years. The bishop, having learned of his constancy, sent him (his) blessing and (his) absolution. Then, his brother Jacob came to him, became a monk, and led a great (spiritual) combat until he had defeated Satan.

Then Abba Matthew went to the monastery of Abba Anthony and remained (there) fighting much (spiritually). There was a righteous man there by the name of Jacob, he saw a vision: as a luminous angel came to Abba Matthew, embraced him, placed the Gospel on his head, and put keys in his hand. (Once) awake, Abba Jacob told this to Abba Matthew. From that moment, Abba Matthew was traveling from city to city, hating vain glory. One day, while he was officiating at the consecration of the Eucharist, Abba Matthew saw our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! - installed on the altar, he was lying on the paten like a small child. At the moment of the breaking of the host, (Matthew) stood for a long time, having extended his hands, stunned and amazed, in great tears and groans (and) in fear; he told this vision to no one. When his master saw (this), he was astonished that he stood for (such) a long time; then he prayed to God to reveal to him (the meaning of) this matter. One day, they entered (the church) according to their habit to consecrate the Eucharist and our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! - appeared to them, lying on the paten like a small child. He was broken, resembling bread, by the hand of the saint Abba Matthew. Then (the master of the latter?) was stunned and said: "Glory to you, my Lord, who has given us this grace, (to us) humans!" When Abba Matthew saw this, he brought iron nails and drove them into his knees every Friday until a worm came out of them.
እምኔሁ ፡ ዕፄ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቀስቋም ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ሀየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ብዙኃ ፡፣ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ያንቅዓዱ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይኔጽሮ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ነቢሮ ፡ በየማነ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይኔጽር ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ይሬ እዮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀላያት ፡ በዕበየ ፣ ስብሐቲሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ለአባ፣ ማቴዎስ ፡ህየንቴሁ ፡ ሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ዓበየ ፡ ረሰዩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዐቃቤ ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ነገርዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ዘርእዩ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡፤ ጵጵስና ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አባ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡፤ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየኃድግዎ ፡ ወነ ሥአ ፡ መላፄ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ወሰሐባ ፡ ለልሳኑ ፡ ወመተራ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ 'ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወቀብአቶ ፡ ቅብዓ ፡ ፈውስ ፤ ወሜጠት ፡ ልሳኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወወሰድዎ ፡ ኅበ” ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወአመ ፡ ሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ወኒለወርኃ ፡ ነኀሴ ፡ በበዓለ ፣ ፍልሰታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡፡'ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ?" ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ይደልዎ ። ወአምጽኡ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ይንበሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕፅኑ ፡ ወሰረረት ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ወሰዓመት ፡ አፉሁ ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ኢነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አትሮንሱ ፡ በጊዜ ፤ ሩርባን ፡ ወኢይበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ በዓርብ ፡ ወበረቡዕ ፤ መኑሂ ፤ እምሰብእ ። ወተነበየ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይነግሥ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ነጊሦ ፡ እሁ ፡ "ውድማ ፡ አስፊሬድ ።
Then he went to the monastery of Qusqam and remained there fighting much (spiritually) so that, when he looked into the air, he saw our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! - seated at the right hand of his father; and, when he looked down, he saw him in the depths in the greatness of his glory. Then, when Abba Gabriel the patriarch died, they appointed Abba Matthew patriarch in his place against his will. As he refused, they assigned a guard to him. The next day, all the bishops told him that they had had a vision according to which he was worthy of the patriarchate. Hearing (this), Abba Matthew knew that they would not leave him; he took a razor in secret, pulled out his tongue, and cut it; that night, our Lady the holy Virgin Mary mother of God came to him, she anointed him with a healing oil and made his tongue as it was before. They brought him to Alexandria and, when they invested him with the patriarchate on the sixteenth of the month of Nahasë during the feast of the translation of our Lady Mary mother of God, a voice was heard from heaven which said: "He is worthy, he is worthy, he is worthy!" They brought the head of Mark the evangelist to place it on his knees, but the head flew up and kissed his mouth. From that moment, he did not sit (anymore) on his throne at the moment of the offertory; no man entered his house on Friday and Wednesday. He prophesied that David, king of Ethiopia, would reign, while his brother Wedem Asfarë was reigning.


[p. 40] {#po.45.p40}
ወገብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፤ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ በፈውሶ” ፡ ድውያን ፡ ወበአንሥኦ” ፣ ሙታን ፡ በሰበን ፡ ዘዲበ ፤ ርእሱ ፤ ወበመዋዕሊሁ?፤ ፣ ተሠይመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እኩይ ፡ እምታሕተ ፡ ሥልጣነ” ፡ ምስር ፡ ወአግበሮ?'" ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ለነሚአ ፡ ፀባሕት ፤ ዘኢነበረ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወሶበ ፡ ዓበዮ ፡ ዘበጦ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት”" ፣ ዝብጠተ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 'ማኅደሩ ፡ ጸዊሮሙ'” ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፣ ሰአላ ፡ አባ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ 'ወላዲተ ፤ አምላክ5 ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሣእ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ እምፃዕረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። ወይእቲኒ ፤ አስተርአየቶ፤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ኢትኅዝን ፡ እስመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ቿዕለት ፡ ተዓርፍ ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ አባ ፡ማቴዎስ ፡ ለኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ለቀሳውስት ፡ ወለዲያቆናት ፤ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ዕረፍቶ ፡ ወመዓዶሙ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወባረኮሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸንዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሕማም ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ ለሥዕለ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ቅድስት፣፤ ድንግል ፡ በክልኤ። ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ነዋኃ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ወሰዓማ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሥዕል ፡ ወዓተበ ፡ ገጾ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወባረከ ፡ ሀገሮ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ አለ ፡ ምእመናን ፡ በእግዚእነ ፤ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እቱድ ። በረከተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፤ ቅድስት ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡፤ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡
This father performed many miracles in his lifetime, healing the sick and raising the dead with the diadem that (was) on his head. In his time a wicked governor was appointed under the authority of the sultan of Egypt. He (wanted) to force the patriarch to levy a tax that did not exist before. As he refused (it) to him, he (had) the patriarch administered a great beating and they brought him back to his dwelling by carrying him. That night, Abba Matthew asked our Lady the holy Virgin Mary mother of God to remove his soul from the torments of this world. She appeared to him saying: "Do not be afflicted, for in eight days you will die." The next day, Abba Matthew summoned the bishops, the priests, the deacons, and all the people, announced his death to them, exhorted them with the word of the faith, and blessed them. When the illness weighed heavily on him, he took the image of our Lady holy, twice virgin, Mary; he prayed for a long time, kissed the image, made the sign of the cross on his face, blessed his city and all the people who believed in our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! - and died on Sunday. May the blessing of his holy prayer be with our king Iyasu for ever and ever.]

[On this day also there is Yarenyos and many men and women, Longinus of Rome and Alexandra who visited Abba Arsenius. May God have mercy through their prayer on our king Iyasu for ever and ever.] <On this day also died the holy father, our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus. May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear Weddase Maryam for ever and ever.> READING FOR THE 6TH OF TERR In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day our Lord entered the house of circumcision and fulfilled the precept of the Law, as says the apostle Paul, tongue of balm: Christ received circumcision in his flesh so that the promise of the fathers might be fulfilled. Indeed, it had seemed to many that our Lord Christ had not entered the house of circumcision and had not been circumcised. If it had been as those thought, the Jews would not have found a greater reason against him than this. The holy Gospel says: The Jews sought false testimony against him but they did not find (any). But our Lord Christ fulfilled


[p. 42] {#po.45.p42}
ሕገ ፡፤ ሙሴ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ግዝረት ፡ በከመ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ለነ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወበልዓ ፡ ናእተ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ለነ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፤ ቅዱሰ ፡ ወደሞ ፡ ክቡረ ። "ወአክበራ ፡ ለቀዳሚት ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ለነ ፡ ዕለተ፤፤፤፣ እቱድ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ፅለተ ፤ ትንሣኤሁ ፡ ቅድስት ። እስመ ፡ ሶበ ፡ከነ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ፳ መዓልት ፡ እምልደቱ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ አመ፣ ሄለጥር ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ሖር ፡ አምጽኦ ፡ 'ለገባሪ ፡ ጠቢብ?!" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግዝሮ ፡ ለወልድየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወንስምዮ ፡ ኢየሱስ ። ወሖረ ፡ዮሴፍ ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ጠቢበ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጠቢብ" ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕፅነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ንጽሕት ። ወይብሎሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ አኃዝቓዎ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ ከመ፤" ፡ እክል ፡ እግዝሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይቁስል!‹ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦጠቢብ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ እርአይ ፡ ኃይለከ ፡ ወጥበበከ ፤ ወግብረከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትግዝረኒ ፡ ወኢይውሕዝ ፡፤ እምኔየ ፡ ደም ፡ 'እስመ ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውሐዝ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ደም፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ደም ፡ ዘይውሕዝ ፡ እምገቦየ ፡ ዘየማን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይረግዙኒ ፡ በኩናት ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ቀራንዮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፅፀ ፣ መስቀል ። ወይውሕዝ?" ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ደም ፡ ወይወርድ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይከውን ፡ መድኃኒቱ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ለሕፃን ፤ ኢየሱስ ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ መልያጽያቶ ፡ ወንዋዮ ፡ ዘሐዊን ፣ ወአግብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቶሙ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ ኢየሱስ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ተመስወ ፡ መላጽያት ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማይ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ወቡርክት ፡ አንቲ ፡ ቡርክት ፡ አምአንስት ፡ 'ወቡሩክ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ከርሥኪ' ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ እምውሉደ?፤፤ ፡ አዳም፤ ፡ አላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ እስመ፣ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተአምራተ ፤ ወውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ምንት ፡ ይከውን ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ ሶበ፤ ፣ ይልህቅ ፡ ዮጊ" ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ማስያስ ፡፤ ዘይሰመይ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘተነበዩ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ነቢያት ፤ ወይቤሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡፣ ድንግል ፡ ትፀንስ ፤ ወትወልድ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ስሙ ፡ አማኑኤል ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሁ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌነ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ትገዝረ ኒኑ ፡ አው ፡ አልቦ ፡ ወሚመ ፡ እግበር ፡ አነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ አበዊየ ፡ 'ወአበወ ፡ አበዊየ፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ መኑ ፡ አቡከ ፡ ወአበ ፤ አቡከ፤0 ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ከርስቶስ ፤ እሙንቱ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ፡ ለሕ ዝብ ። ወእሙንቱ ፡ ቀዳሚ-፥፤ ፣ ዘወሀቦሙ ፤ እግዚአብሔር?" ፡ ግዝረተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ እትናገር ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አንሥአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ 'ኅበ ፣
all the law of Moses, he entered the house of circumcision according to the Law and instituted for us in its (= circumcision's) place Christian baptism. He ate the unleavened bread and instituted for us in its place his holy body and his glorious blood. He observed the first sabbath (= Saturday) and instituted for us Sunday which is the day of his holy resurrection. Indeed, when eight days had passed for our Lord Christ since his birth - that is to say the sixth of Terr -, Mary the pure said to Joseph the righteous: "Go, bring a skilled practitioner so that he may circumcise my son according to the law of Moses and let us call him Jesus." Joseph went and brought a doctor. When the doctor had arrived, he found our Lord Christ in the lap of the pure Virgin Mary. The doctor said to them (= to the parents): "Hold the child, so that I may be able to circumcise him, so that I do not injure (him)." The child Jesus answered and said to him: "Doctor, I want to see your power, your skill, and your way of proceeding to circumcise me. The blood will not flow from me, for it is not fitting that blood should flow from me in this world with the exception of the blood that will flow from my right side when they pierce me with a lance at the place of the Skull on the wood of the cross. The blood will flow from me, will fall to the ground, and will be the salvation of Adam and of the whole universe." When the doctor heard the speech of the child Jesus, he gathered his razors and (his) iron instruments, put them back in their case, arose, and prostrated himself at the feet of the child Jesus. Then the razors liquefied and became like water. The doctor said to Mary: "Pure and blessed are you among women, and blessed the fruit of your womb, for this child is not part of the children of Adam but he is God: if he performs this miracle while he is a child, what will become of him when he has grown! Perhaps this (is) the messiah who is called Christ, about whom the prophets prophesied and said: Behold, a virgin will conceive and give birth to a son and he will be called by the name of Emmanuel which, translated, (means) 'God with us'." Our Lord Christ answered and said to him: "Behold, it is I, do you circumcise me or not? Or must I do, myself, as my fathers and the fathers of my fathers have done?" The doctor said to him: "Who (are) your father and the father of your father?" Our Lord Christ said to him: "They are Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob, the fathers of the people (of Israel). [It is (to) them first that God gave circumcision." The doctor said to him: "I cannot speak with you, for the Holy Spirit (is) with you." Then our Lord Christ raised his eyes to heaven toward his divinity (?) and said: "Father, give me the circumcision that you once gave to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob without the hand of man." Immediately, the circumcision appeared on him without the hand of man. The circumcision of


[p. 44] {#po.45.p44}
መለኮቱ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ ግዝረተ ፡ ዘወሀብኮሙ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ ወለይስሐቅ ፡ ወለያዕቆብ ፡ እምቀዳሚ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እደ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ግዝ ረት ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ እደ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወኮነት ፡ ግዝረቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ዘኢይትዓወቅ ፡ በከመ ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ ፀአቱ ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወኢማሰነ ፡ ድንግልናሃ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ በዐቱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ጽዮን ፡'ወአናቅጽ ፡ ዕፅዋት፥ ። ወበከመ ፡ ከሠተ፣ ክሂሎቶ ፡ በዘፈቀደ ፡ ኪነ፥" ፡ ጥበቡ ፣ ከማሁ ፡ ኢይትመተር ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ በግዝረት ። ወሶበ ፡ አኮ ፡ ፈቃዱዐ ፡ ደም ፡ ወማይ ፡ ዘውሕዘ ፡ እምገ ቦሁ ፡ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅኖ ፡ ለአዳም፤ ፡ 'እመ ፡ ኢውሕዘ ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢይኩን ፡ ውሱከ ፡ ወኢሕጹጸ ፡ በርእሱ ፡ አላ ፡ "ኮነ ፡ ፈቃዱ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈ
our Lord is not elucidated, just as the mystery of his exit from the womb of Mary: he did not destroy her virginity; just as his coming to the apostles in the room of Zion while the doors (were) closed. And just as he revealed his power in that which the operation of his wisdom willed, so nothing was taken from his body in the circumcision. If it had not been his will, the blood and water that flowed from his side upon the wood of the cross to save 'Adäm would not have flowed from him, so that he would be neither increased nor diminished in his person, but it was his will so that he might fulfill the law that he had formerly enacted.
ጽም ፡ ሕገ ፡ ዘአዘዘ ፡ እምቀዳሚ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ዞጊዜ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አማን፥፣ አንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ "ሕያው ፡ ንጉሠ" ፡ እስራኤል ። ወሖረ፥ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካኑ ፡ ወአኃዘ ፡ ይንግሮሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ወዘሰምዓ ። ወለእግዚእነ፥ ፣ 'ወአምላክነ ፤ ወመድኃኒነ" ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ ስብሐት" ፤ ወሰጊድ ፡ ወለአቡሁ ፣ ጌር ፡ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማሕየዊ‹ ፡
When the physician saw this miracle and heard his speech, he was greatly astonished and prostrated himself three times at the feet of our Lord Christ and said: "In truth, you are the Son of the living God, the king of Israel." The physician went to his home and began to tell the children of Israel what he had seen and what he had heard. To our Lord, our God, and our Savior Jesus Christ be glory and adoration, as well as to his excellent Father and to the life-giving Holy Spirit, now, forever, and unto the ages of ages, amen!
ይእዜኒ ፡ ወዘልፈኒ ፡ ወለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኖኅ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዓሥር ፡ ትውልድ ፡ እምነ፤" ፡ አዳም ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ኖኅ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ይኔጽር ፡ በምሥጢር ፡ አምላ ካዊ ፤ ከመ ፡ ይበዝኅ ፤ ኃጢአት ፡ ወይመልእ ፡ እኩይ፥ ፡ ወይውሕዱ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወይደመስስ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ በማየ ፡ አይኅ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይላሑ ፡ ወይበኪ ፡ ወይቄጽር ፡ ገጾ ፡ ወዓቀበ ፤ ድንግልናሁ ፡ ፳፻ ዓመተ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትለአኮ ፡፤ ለሥጋ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አዳም ፡፤ ወለትእዛዘ ፡ አምላክ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያውስብ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደመስስ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ በማየ ፡ አይኅ ፡ ወአ ልቦ ፡ ዘይተርፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ዚአሁ ። ወአውሰበ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ዘስማ ፡ ሐይከል ፡ ወለተ ፡ አቤኔዜር" ። ወቦአ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ይጊዜያተ ፡ ወወለደቶሙ ፡ ለሴም ፡ ወለካም ፡ ወለያፌት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለኖኅ ፡ ግዝም ፡ ፅፀወ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወግበር ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ዘኑጐ ፡ ፻፻ በእመት ፡ ወግድሙ ፡ ፳፻በእመት ፤ ወቆሙሂ ፡ በእመት ። ወግበር ፡ ውስቴተ ፡ ፻ ወዓርጋተ ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ መዓርግ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ለእንስሳ ፡ ወዳግማዊ፤ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ለአዕዋፍ ፡ ወለኩሉ ፡ ዘይትሐወስ ፤ ወሣልሳዊ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለውሉዱኒ ፤ ወለአንስቲያሆሙኒ ። ወገብረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ሙዳየ ፡ማይ ፡ ወመዝገበ ፡ እክል ፡ ለሲሳዮሙ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ መጥቅዓ ፡ እምዕፀ ፡ ከስያስ፡ ፡ ዘኑ ፡ ፻በእመት ፤ ወግድሙ ፡ በእመት ፡፤ ወመንፈቀ ፡ እመት ፡ በዘቦቱ ፡ ይጠቅዕ ፡ ጊዜያተ ፡ በመዓልት ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ በነግህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ መስተገብራን ፡ ለግብር ፡
[On this day also died our father Noh (= Noah) who is the tenth generation from 'Adäm (= Adam). This Noh, from his childhood, saw by a divine mystery that sin was multiplying, (that) evil was increasing, (that) the righteous were decreasing in number, and (that) all that (was) flesh would be destroyed by the water of the flood. Because of this, he lamented, wept, and had a closed face. He kept his virginity for five years and remained serving the body of our father 'Adäm (= Adam) and the commandments of God. Then God ordered him to take a wife and announced to him that all that (was) flesh would be destroyed by the water of the flood and (that) nothing would remain on earth except his (= Noh's) race. He married a woman by the name of Haykal, daughter of 'Abênezer. He went in unto her three times and she bore Sëm (= Shem), Käm (= Ham), and Yäfet (= Japheth). Then God said to Noh: "Cut wood from this holy mountain and make an ark whose length (shall be) three hundred cubits, the width five hundred cubits, and the height thirty cubits. Make three stories in it; let the first story be for the animals, let the second be for the birds and for everything that creeps." (He said to him also) that the third was to be for him (= Noh), as well as for his sons and for their wives. (Noh) made in it a cistern for water and a granary for their food; he also made a trumpet of kasyäs-wood whose length (was) three cubits and the width one and a half cubits, to play it three times a day: the first, in the morning, so that the workers would gather for
