አስተርአየ ፡ ሎሙ ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘወደይኩ ፡ ፍትወተ ፡፤ ዝሙት ፤ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ አዋልደ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እስከ ፡ ረሰያክሙ፤፤ ፡ ነገሥተ ። ወለእመ ፡ ሰማዕክሙ ፡ ትእዛዝየ ፡ አነ፤፤ ፡ እሬስዮሙ፤ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ፤፤፤ ፡ ውስተ፣፤ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ፡ ይትኤዘዙ ፡ ለክሙ፤፡ ። ወእምይእዜ ፡ ድንኑ ፡ ወስግዱ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወግ በሩ ፤ እምወርቅ ፡ ወእምብሩር ፡ አምሳላተ ፡ 'ወስምይዎሙ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ አጵሎን ፤ ወአርዳሚስ፤፤ ፡ 'ወተአዝዝዎሙ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ለሰብእ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግሩ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዕጣነ ፤ ወይስግዱ ፤ ሎሙ፤፤ ፣ ወትእፅዉ፤ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ወታር ሕዉ፤። ፡ አብያተ ፡ ጣዖታት ። ወተአዝዝዎሙ፤ ፡ ለሹዙሎሙ ፡ 'እለ ፡ ውስተ"። 0፣ መንግሥትክሙም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘኢይትኤዘዝ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ወኢይሰ ግድ ፤ ለአማልክቲክሙ ፡፣ ኩንንዎ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ወይሙት ፡ በእኩይ ፡ ሞት ፤ ወይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፤ በሊሕ ። ወአውሥእዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንትኤዘዝ ፤ ለከ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤለነ ፡ ወሰገዱ፤"0 ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወገብሩ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፤ በሓውርተ ፤ መንግሥቶሙ ። ወሜምዎ ፡ ለ፳ዘስሙ ፣፤ ህርማንዮስ፤፤ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ደወላ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ለአርያኖስ ፣፤ መኩንነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ አንዴናው ፡ ወላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአዘዝዎሙ ፡
emperors. If you obey my orders, I will make all the men who (are) on all the earth obey you. Henceforth, bow down and prostrate yourselves before me, make simulacra of gold and silver, and name them the gods Apelon (= Apollo) and Ardamis (= Artemis). Order the people to offer them incense and to prostrate themselves before them; close the churches and open the temples of idols. Order all those who (are) in your empire to do this; whoever does not obey you and does not prostrate himself before your gods, make him undergo great torture, let him die a bad death, and let his head be cut off with a sharp sword." They answered him and said to him: "We will obey you according to (that) which you have said," and they prostrated themselves then. They did everything according to what Satan had ordered them, in all the regions of their empire. They established a (man) by the name of Hermanyos (= Arminius) governor of the city of Alexandria and of all its district, and they established Aryanos (= Arianus) governor of the city of Andenaw (= Antinoë) and of Upper Egypt. They ordered them to apply these (measures) in all the cities and villages.
ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር፤ ፡ ወአዕጸዳት፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን፤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዜና ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወተሀብአ፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይጤይቅ ፡ ዘይከውን ፤ ። ወጸውዖ ፡ ለዐርኩ ፡ ለቴዎድሮስ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ "ወተናገሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ!" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘሰምዑ ፡ ወሐዘኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወኮኑ ፣ ያበዝጐ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ወያስተበሞዑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅኖሙ ፡ እምእ ኩይ!» ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን፤ ፣ ይዌስክ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ሠናየ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡፤ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሰይጣናት፤" ፡ ነገርዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ!፤ ፣ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ዘስሙ ፣ ቢፋሞን! ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ውሲም ፡ ወእምደወለ ፡ምስር ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡መሰርይ ፤ ወይትዐደው!፤ ፡ ትእዛዛተ ፡ ንጉሥ፤ ፡ ወይረግም ፡ አማልክተ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ወፈነዋ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አርያ ኖስ ፡፤ አዘዞ፤ ፡ ከመ ፤ ይንሥኦ ፡ ለቅዱስ፤ ፡ ወይኩንኖ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይወግር፤ ፡ ፅጣነ ፤ ለአጵሎን ፣ ወለአርድሚስ፤ ፣ ወለእመ ፣ ኢሰምዓ፤ ምትር፤" ፣ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡፤ መልአከ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን፤፡፤ ፡ ወነገሮ፤። ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'ሀለዎ ፡ ይኩን፤፥ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ወእሙ ፡ ወአስተርአዮ፤፤ ፣ አክሊለ ፡ ዘአስተዳለወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወለእሙ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ለድዮጋኖስ፤፤፤ ፡ ገብሩ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ፣ ቢፋሞን ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እሙ ፡ ወነገራ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ፤ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ካዕበ! ፡ ነገረቶ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ፤፤ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ራእየ፤ ፡ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ኅቡረ፤ ።
When Abba Bifamon learned this news, he was much afflicted and hid himself until he knew what was going to happen. He called his friend Tewodros (= Theodore) one day, and they talked about what they had learned; they were much afflicted together, they multiplied prayers and supplicated God so that he might preserve them from evil; the saint Abba Bifamon practiced good all the more. His fame reached the impious emperor Maksemyanos through the mediation of demons. They spoke to him and said to him: "A man whose name (is) Bifamon, from the city of Wesim, of the district of Mesr, (is) a Christian magician; he transgresses the orders of the emperor and curses the gods." Then the emperor wrote a letter and sent it to Aryanos; he ordered him (in it) to take the saint and to torture him until he offered incense to Apelon and to Ardemis; "if he does not obey (,he said,) cut off his head with the sword." The angel of God appeared to the saint Abba Bifamon, told him how things had happened, informed him that it fell to him to be a martyr—he and his mother—and showed him the crown that he had prepared for him and for his mother. He ordered him to take with him Dyoganos (= Diogenes) his servant. Immediately, the saint Abba Bifamon rose, entered to his mother, and told her everything just as he had seen (it); she told him in her turn that she had seen this vision; they rejoiced together. The saint


[p. 200] {#po.45.p200}
ወሖረ ፡'ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን፤፤ ፡'ኀበ ፡ ማኅደሩ!። ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ይጴሊ፤ ፡ እስከ ፣ ጽባሕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ካዕበ፤፤። ፣ ኀበ ፡ ዓርኩ ፣፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወበጽሐ፤ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወነገሮ፤ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አምድኅረ ፡ የኀልፍ ፡ መዋፅለ ፡ ስደት ፡ አንተ ፡ ትሠየም ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአዘዞ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕንጽ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ተኣምሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወተፈልጡ!“ ።
Abba Bifamon went to his dwelling and stood to pray until the morning. When it was morning, he dispatched again to his friend Tewodros, and (the latter) came to him. (Bifamon) told him (the vision) as he had seen (it) and said to him: "After the days of the persecution have passed, you will be named bishop of this city." And he prescribed to him to build a church for him; then they took leave of one another and separated.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ውሲም፣ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ለልሂቃነ ፡ሀገር ፤ ወተስእሎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቢፋሞን፤” ፡ እስመ ፡ኮነ፣ ፍቁረ ፤ በኀቤሆሙ!?፤ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ ተንሥአ ፡ አባ ፣ ቢፋሞን ፡ ወለብሰ ፡ አልባሳተ ፡ ሠናያተ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ነዋኃ፤ ፡ ወተኣምኖሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፣ ቤቱ ፡ ወተጽዕነ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፈረስ፤” ፡ ፀዓዳ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይነብሩ፤” ፡ ልሂቃነ ፡ ሀገር፣ ጉቡኣን ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ አክበርዎ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አርያኖስ ። ወሶበ ፣ ርእዮ ፤ አርያኖስ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡፤ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን፤"?-፤" ፣ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወተኣምኖፕ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ሎቱ፤?" ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ። ወተሰጥዎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለም ንት ፤ ትብለኒ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወትርጓሜሁ ፡ ለሰላም ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወበአማን ፡ እትፌሣሕ ፤ ወእትሐሠይ ፡ 'እስመ ፡ አሐውር!?" ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ወመድኃኒየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ዘበእንቲአከ፤ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፍ፤ ፡ 'እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ለዓላዊ፤፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢመጸእኩ፤። ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንግረኒ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ኅሠመ ፡፣ አላ፤ ፡ መጸእኩ ፡ ኀቤከ፤ ፡ በመጽሐፈ ፡ መልእክቱ ፤ ለንጉሥ፤፤ ፣ ከመ ፤ ትሥዕ ፣ ለአማልክት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐሰ ፤ ለእግዚአብሔር፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ እስግድ፤ ፡ ለተግባረ ፡ እደ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እስመ ፡ አማልክቲሆሙ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ። ግብረ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ አፈ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢይነቡ ፡ አዕይንተ፤ ፡ ወኢይሬእዩ ፡ እዝነ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢይሰምኡ ፡ 'አንፈ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢያፄንዉ፤ ፡ እደ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢይገሥሥሠ ፡ እግረ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢየሐውሩ፤» ፡ ለይኩን፤፤ ፡ ገባሪሆሙ ፡ ከማሆሙ ። ወአንሰ" ፡ ኢይሰግድ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፥፤ ለእግዚእየ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ፤ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኩነኖ፤ ፡ በዘዘዚአሁ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወአሰሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዘነበ ፡ ፈረስ ፡ ወአሮጸ፤ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዐውደ፤ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ሀገር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ እሙ ፡ ወአግብርቲሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኅቡረ፣ "ወእሙንቱ ፡ ፳፻ አግብርት! ። ወገሠጾሙ ፡ ቅዱስ! ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ወእሙ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ 'በብዙኅ ፡፣ ነገር፤ ፡ እምወንጌል ፤ ቅዱስ""0 ፡ ወከልሑ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፥፡ "እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ፤ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ክርስቲያን"" ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ወገፍትዑ ፡ መንበሮ ፡ ለአርያኖስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክርዩ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዓዘቅተ ፡ ዓባየ ፡ ወይምልእዋ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ወይውግር
A few days later, Aryanos the governor arrived at the city of Wesim. He brought the chiefs of the city and questioned them about Bifamon, for (the latter) was loved by them. At that moment, Abba Bifamon rose and put on beautiful clothes; he said a long prayer and took leave of all the people of his house. He mounted a white horse and went to where the chiefs of the city were gathered. When they saw him, they honored him and went with him to Aryanos. When Aryanos saw the saint Abba Bifamon, he rejoiced because of him, greeted him, rose for him, and said to him: "Greetings to you." The saint answered him and said to him: "Why do you say 'greetings' to me? The meaning of 'greetings' is 'joy,' and, in truth, I rejoice and exult, for I am going to go to the Lord my God and my Savior Jesus Christ. It is regarding you that the Scripture said: 'There is no joy for the impious.'" Aryanos said to him: "I have not come to you so that you might say insults to me, but I have come to you because of a letter from the emperor so that you might sacrifice to the gods." The saint said to him: "Far be it from God that I worship the work of the hand of men! As the prophet says: The gods of the nations (are) of gold and silver; the works of the hands of men have a mouth and do not speak, eyes and do not see, they have ears and do not hear, they have a nose and do not smell, they have hands and do not touch, they have feet and do not walk. May their author be like them. As for me, I worship only my Lord Jesus Christ." When Aryanos heard this discourse from him, he was very irritated against him and (had) him tortured with various torments: he (had) him tied to the tail of a horse and made him run with it around the whole city. When his mother and his servants learned this, they came to him all together—the servants were five hundred. The saint admonished them and taught them—and his mother was with them—with many words of the holy Gospel. All cried out, saying: "We are Christians openly!" and they overturned the seat of Aryanos; (the latter) ordered to


[p. 202] {#po.45.p202}
ዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡'እንዘ ፡ ሕያዋን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ በከመ፣ አዘዘ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን ፡ ያጸንፆሙ ፡ ወይሜህሮሙ ፡ ወያስተዔግሦሙ ፣ እስከ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ስምዖሙ ። ወእሙሰ ፡ ሰአለቶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ" ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወዓተበ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ሖሪ ፡ በሰላም ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ገደፈት ፡ ነፍሳ ፤ ውስተ ፡፣ እሳት ፡፣ ወነሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እሉ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዓቀቦ ፣ ለድዩጋኖስ፤0 ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ መልእክተ ፣፤ እግዚኡቶ «
dig a large pit for them, to fill it with fire, and to throw them all into it alive; they did to them according to (that) which he had ordered. The saint Abba Bifamon comforted them, taught them, and exhorted them to constancy, (and this) until they had accomplished their martyrdom. The mother of the saint asked him to pray for her; he made the sign of the cross over her and said to her: "Go in peace." Immediately, she threw herself into the fire and received the crown of martyrdom with these martyrs. God preserved Dyuganos, his (= Bifamon's) servant, so that he might ensure the service of his master.
ወፈነዎ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን"" ፡ ኀበ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ። "ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ0 ፣ ይቤሎ ፡ አንተኒ ፡ ቢፋሞንቦ ፣ መሠርይ ፡ ዘተዐደውከ ፡ ትእዛዛቲነ፤ ፡ ኢሰገድከ ፡ ለአማልክቲነ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይሰግድ፤፡፤፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ለንጉሥየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአንተሰ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገሃነም ። ወተምዕዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ብዙኃ፤፤። ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ህርማንዮን፤፤ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አርያኖስ ።
Aryanos sent the saint Abba Bifamon to the emperor Maksemyanos. When (the latter) saw him, he said to him: "Are you Bifamon the magician who has transgressed our orders (and) has not worshipped our gods?" The saint said to him: "I, I worship only my King and my God Jesus Christ; as for you, you will go to hell." The emperor was irritated against him and made him undergo many tortures. When he was weary of torturing him, he sent him to Hermanyon, and this one sent him to Aryanos. The saint neither ate nor drank while he was making the journey.
ወቅዱስሰ ፤ ኢበልዓ፤ ፣ ወኢሰትየ"፤፤ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ንብረቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡፤ ፍኖት፤፤ ። ወአርያኖስሰ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወቀነዎ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ፤፤' ፡ በቅንዋተ ፡ ሐጻውንት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢሰገድከ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ አነ ፡ ኣጠፍአከ ፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ፀረፈ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ 'ወላዕለ ፡ አማልክቲሁ ፡ ወረገሞሙ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ፣ ይስሐብዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 'ዙላ ፡ ሀገር፤ ፡ ወያውዕይዎ ፡ በእሳት ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንዴ ናው ፡ ወገብሩ ፤ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወአውፅኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና፤፡ « ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፣ ይቀውም ፡ ውሕዘ ፤ እምእገሪሁ ፡ ደም ፡ ብዙኅ፡፡፡ ። ወሀሎ ፡ በህየ ፡ 8ብእሲ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ፅውር ፣ ወለምጽ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ደም ፤ ወወሀብዎ"፡፤ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዕፅውር፡ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ። ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ በአዕይንቲሁ፤፤ ፡ ወነጽሐ ፡ እምለምጹ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወጸርሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡'ይትአመን ፡ ወይቤ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ገሃደ ፣ ኣአምን ፡፣ በክርስቶስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፣ አርያኖስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ 'ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ" ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ፤ ። ወጸውዖ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን ፤። ፡ ለድዩጋኖስ፤፤፤፣ ገብሩ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀብ፤፤ ፣፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ 'ወይዕቀብ ፡ ደሞ”፡፣ በስንዶናት ፡-ወይንግር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ለመሃይምናን” ፡ እለ ፡ በሀገሩ ፡ ውሲም ፡ እስመ ፡ሀሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ የኃልፍ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ። ወዐተበ፤ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መሰቀል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፤ አምላክ ። ወቀርበ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ግበሩ ፡ ዘተአዘዝክሙ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካን ፤ ባሕቲቶሙ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፣ ስምፅ ፡ ዘኢይማስን ። ወውሕዘ ፡ እምክሳዱ ፡ ደም ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡ ገብሩ ፤
Aryanos took the saint and (had) him nailed to the hands and feet with iron nails. He said to him: "If you do not worship the gods, I will make you perish." The saint insulted him and his gods and cursed them. (Aryanos) ordered him to be dragged through the whole city and to be burned outside the city of Andenaw, and they did this to him. Our Lord brought him out of the fire unharmed. When the saint was standing, much blood flowed from his feet. A man was standing there with the crowd; he was blind and his body leprous. They took of this blood and gave it to the blind man; (the latter) put (it) on his eyes and on his whole body: his eyes saw and he was immediately purified of his leprosy. He cried out, making a profession of faith, and said: "In the name of Jesus Christ, I am a Christian openly; I believe in Christ." Aryanos ordered his head to be cut off; they cut off his head with a sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom. The saint Abba Bifamon called Dyuganos his servant and ordered him secretly to guard his body, to guard his blood in a fine linen cloth, and to tell his whole life to the believers who (were) in the city of Wesim, for the time of the persecution was to pass. He made the sign of the cross over himself, saying: "In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God." He approached the soldiers and said to them: "Do what you have been ordered." The soldiers took him to a place (where they were) alone, cut off his head with a sword, and he received the unfading crown of martyrdom. Much blood flowed from his neck; his servant


[p. 204] {#po.45.p204}
ድዩጋኖስ፤ ፡ ስንዶናተ ፡ ዚአሁ፤፡ ፡ ወተቀበለ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ቅዱስ። ወመልአ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ጹና ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወደንገፁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፤ ወወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ፍርሀት ፡ ዓቢይ ።
Dyuganos stretched out his fine linen cloth and collected his holy blood in it. The place was filled with a very sweet odor, so much so that the soldiers were astonished, were greatly amazed, and a great fear descended upon them.
"ወሶበ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ሐራ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወገነዙ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ 'በግንዘታት ፡ ንጹሓት” ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፡ ዕፍረተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወገነዙ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወዘመሩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ 'ዝማሬ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ወጾርዎ፤" ፡ ኀበ ፣ ምዕራብ ፤፤ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ በህየ ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ እመቃብሪሁ ፡፤ ተኣምራት ፤ ብዙኃት ። ወገብሩሰ ፤ ለቅዱስ ፤ ነሥኣ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሰንዶናት ፡፤ ወዓቀባ ፣፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ይደነግፅ ፡ ወይ ሔሊ ፡ምንተ ፡ ዘይገብር ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን፤፤”፡፡ ፣ ወአ ዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያይድፅ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለዓርኩ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወለሰብአ ፡ሀገሩ ። ወፈነወ፡ሎቱ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሰብአ ፡ መሃይምናነ ፡ ወጾርዎ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሐመሮሙ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምይእቲ ፡፤ ሰንዶናት ፤ ተኣምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ድዩጋኖስ፤" ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፣ እምነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን፤፤ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ውሲም ፡ ወአይድዖ ፡ ለዓርኩ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢፋሞን ፡ ወመጠዎ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ይእተ ፡ ሰንዶናተ፣፤ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት፤ ። ወኮነት ፡ ይእቲ ፣ ሰንዶናት ፡ ዕቅብት፤" ፡ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፤ ወነግሠ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ወሐልቀ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ህድአት ፡ወሰላም ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡'ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ። ወፈቀደ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡፣ ቦቱ”፣0፣ ተስፋ ፡ ወኑዛዜ ፡ ወኀይለ ፡ ወፈውሰ ፡ ለመሃይምናን”" ። ወኮነ ፡ ስምዑ፤ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ቃው ፡ በላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በደወለ ፡ ጥማ ፡ አመ ፡፣ ፳ወኒለወርኃ ፡ ጥር ። ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ተኣምራተ፤ ፣ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወፈውሰ ፡ ዓቢየ"፤ ። ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ዘበሀገረ ፡ ድምኑ ፡ እስመ፤”፤ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወ፪ለወ ርኃ ፡ ሐምሌ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፤ ኀቤሃ፡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወይከሥት ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡
When the soldiers had left, believers came and buried the body of the saint with his servant in a clean shroud; they brought many perfumes, buried his body with them, and sang many canticles over him; they carried him outside the city, to the west, and buried him there. Many miracles manifested themselves from his tomb. The servant of the saint took the fine linen cloth and kept it with him. When he wanted to go to his city, he began to be troubled and to reflect on what he should do. The saint Abba Bifamon appeared to him and ordered (him) to make his story known to his friend Tewodros and to the people of his city. God sent him believers, and they took him with them on their boat. While he was with them on the boat, God made many miracles appear from this fine linen cloth. The servant Dyuganos told them everything that was the case with the saint Abba Bifamon; they were greatly astonished and glorified God. Then he arrived at his city, Wesim, and made known to his (= Bifamon's) friend Tewodros everything that was the case with the saint Abba Bifamon and gave him the fine linen cloth. Many wonders and miracles manifested themselves from it. The fine linen cloth was kept at their place (= the inhabitants of Wesim) until Diyogletyanos the renegade died and Quastantinos (= Constantine) the righteous emperor reigned. The time of the persecution ended, and tranquility and peace came for the people of the Christians in all the countries. God wanted to reveal the body of the saint so that, through him, there might be hope, consolation, strength, and healing for the believers. His martyrdom took place in the city of Qaw (= Qäou) in Upper Egypt, in the district of Tema, on the twenty-seventh of the month of Terr. God made many miracles and great healings appear from his body, especially in his church which (is) in the city of Demnu (= Demnou), for on the twenty-seventh of the month of Hamle many people gather there, and God reveals there among them many healings through his martyr saint Abba Bifamon. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us forever, amen.
ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ብዙኃ" ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ሰማዕቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ቢፋ ሞን፤?-፡7 ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ታውፍሎንጦስ ፡ ወሶርኔስ"?" ፡ ሰማዕት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also (there is) the commemoration of Taweflontos (= Theophilantius) and of Sornes the martyrs. May their prayer be with us in the centuries of centuries.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ነሥእዎ ፡ ለሄኖክ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ያሬድ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መላልኤል፣ ወልደ ፡ ቃይናን ፡ 'ወልደ ፡ ሄኖስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሴት፤" ፡ ወልደ ፡ አዳም ፡ በሩጸተ፣

took him to heaven and placed him between two spirits. He was called scribe of the command of God. He said: "I have spoken regarding the righteous with the Holy and the Great One who will come out of his dwelling, the God of the world. From there, he will tread upon Mount Sina (= Sinai), he will appear in his army, and, from heaven, he will appear in the strength of his power. All will be afraid" while he will be on the waters of Dan in (the territory of) Dan which is to the south of the west of Armon (= Hermon). He was reading the formula of their prayer until he dozed off. (A voice) called him in a dream, visions assailed him so that he might rebuke the watchers of heaven. He approached a great house which (was) built of hailstones, and the walls of this house (were) like a mosaic. He saw another house which was larger than that one, and all its door (was) open and built of tongues of fire, and it surpassed it in glory and in splendor. He looked and saw a high throne, its appearance (was) like (that of) crystal, and its perimeter like the sun; the voice of the cherubim came out from under the throne. He saw seven mountains, three on the side of the east and three on the side of the south; the one in the middle reached up to heaven. He saw the four wings of the Lord of spirits, and he saw four different faces. He prophesied about Christ, saying: "I saw another walking with the Head of days, and his face had the appearance (of that) of a man, and his face was full of grace like (that of) one of the holy angels." He said again about Christ: "That day, the Son of man was called before the Head of days, before the sun was created and the stars made." He said again: "This one will be a staff for the righteous, a light for the nations, and a hope for those who suffer." He spoke and said about the changing of the luminaries of heaven: "Each (is) according to its kind, according to its power, and according to its name." He spoke about the gates of the winds and said: "(There are) three on the side of the east, three at the west, three at the south, and three at the north." He said about Adam: "Behold, a bull came out of the earth, and this one was white." He said about Hewan (= Eve): "A heifer came out." He said about the Temple: "I watched until he folded this old house, and they removed all the columns and all the beams and ornaments of this house." About the Church, he said: "I watched until the Lord of the sheep brought a new house, larger and higher than the first, and he set it up at (the location of) the first which had been rolled up. All its columns (were) new and its ornaments new; (it was) larger than the first, the old one, which he had removed, and all the sheep were


[p. 208] {#po.45.p208}
አራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወአዕዋፈ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ እስመ ፡ኮኑ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ጌራነ ፡ ወገብኡ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቤት፤ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፤ ምስለ ፤ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
in the midst of it." He said about the faithful sheep: "All these are white, and their fleece (is) thick and pure. All the wild animals which had been scattered and the birds of the heaven gathered in this house, for all were virtuous and had returned to the house." May his prayer and his
blessing be with our king Iyasu in the centuries of centuries.]

### READING OF THE 28 TERR
አመ ፡ ፳ቿለጥር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቀሌምንጦስ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ብአሲት ፡ መበለት ፡ መሃይምንት፤ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኪራስ ፡ ወስማ ፤ አፍሮስያ፤፣ ወበመዋዕሊሆሙ ፡ ለአላራልዮስ ፡ ወሉቅዮስ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ገላትያዊያን ። 'ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ መሀረቶ ፡ እሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡፣ ትምህርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መዋፅሊሁ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመት ፡ ተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በጾም ፤ ወበጸሎታት ፡ 'ብዙኃት ፡ ወተጸምደ ፡ ተፀምዶ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወኮነ ፣ ኢይበልዕ ፤ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ዘያበስልዎ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወኢሥጋ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ አላ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሲሳዩ ፡ ብቁላተ፣ ምድር ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ዲያቀነ ፡ ተወሰከ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ወጥበበ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጸጋ 7፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሁ!፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ፤፤ ፡ ወአብጽሖ፤"፤ ፡ ወጦ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወተካየዶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስዮ፤ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ተአዘዘ፤ ፡ ወሦዓ ፡ ለጣዖታቲሁ፤" ። 'ወሶበ ፡ ኢሰምዓ!፤ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ኩነኖ ፡ በዘዘዚአሁ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወአጽንዖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፡፤ ወአስተኃፈሮሙ!፤!" ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ ወተጐለቄ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ '፳አክሊለ ፤ ስምዕ፤ ፣ በእዴሁ ፤ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ። ወበጽሐ?" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፪ዐውደ ፡ ምዙናናት ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the saint Qalementos (= Clement) became a martyr. This saint was the son of a believing widow of the people of the city of Kiras (= Ancyra)—her name (was) Afrosya (= Euphrasia)—in the time of Alaralyos (= Alarius) and Lugyos (= Lucius), Galatian kings. When the child had grown, his mother taught him all the teaching of the Church. When he was twelve years old, he waged a great (spiritual) combat in fasting and many prayers. He devoted himself to great asceticism: he ate nothing that had been cooked by fire, never meat; his food was the vegetables of the earth. When he was ordained deacon, he grew in science and wisdom; the grace of God was upon him. When his fame was known to the impious emperor Diyogletyanos (= Diocletian), (the latter) had him brought, flattered him much, and promised to make him (his) son if he obeyed him and sacrificed to his idols. As he did not obey (his) order, he made him undergo various tortures. God fortified him in the torture and raised him up unharmed; (the saint) covered them with shame at that moment. A crown of martyrdom was counted to him by the hand of Diyogletyanos. He went to seven (sessions of) tribunal, and they tortured him (there).
ወኩነንዎ ። ዳግማይ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሠ ፤ ሮሜ ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መሠርይ ፡ ዘኢር ኢኩ ፡ዘከማሁ" ፡ በሥራዩ፤ ወኩነኔሰ" ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ወኢከመ ፡ ምንትኒ ። ናሁ ፡ ፈነውኩ ፡ 'ኅቤከ ፡ ዮጊ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'ትጊጦ ፡ ወትሚጥ" ፡ ልቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሠፅ፣ ለአማልክት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አልባሳተ፣ ዘወርቅ ፤ ወኢነሥኦሙ ፡ ወኢነጸረ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኀቤሆሙ፥ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወተምዕዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወኢፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁቶ፥" ። ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ብዙኃ፤፤ ፡ ወቀሠፎ ፡ ወመተሮ ፡ መለያልያቲሁ ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡!'ያጸንዖ ፡ ወያነሥኦ፥" ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀዳሚ። ሣልሣይ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፣ እምኩነኔሁ" ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡
Second martyrdom: When Diyogletyanos was weary of torturing him, he sent him to the governor of Rome (= Rome) and wrote to him (a letter) saying: "This one (is) a magician to whose magic I have (never) seen anything similar; the torture for him is as nothing. Behold, I have sent him to you in the hope that you will flatter him and convert his heart so that he might sacrifice to the gods." When (Qalementos) arrived before the governor of Rome, they brought him clothes (brocaded) with gold, but the saint did not take them nor even look at them. (The governor) was very irritated against him, but (Qalementos) was not afraid of it. (The governor) made him undergo many tortures, he (had) him scourged and cut his limbs, and God fortified him and raised him up unharmed without damage, as he was before. Third martyrdom: When the governor was weary of torturing him, he sent him


[p. 210] {#po.45.p210}
ዞ.=1 ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኒቆምድያቶ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተኃፍረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ጸላእያን ፡ ወያነሥኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ።
to Maksemyanos (= Maximian) in the city of Nigomedya (= Nicomedia). (The latter) made him undergo great torture until he was covered with shame, he and all the enemies (of the saint); God raised the latter up without damage.
ራብዕ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ ሜጦ ፡ 'ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ፤" ፡ ኪራስ ፡ 'ዘተሐጽነ ፡ ቦቱ፥ጾ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ"።
Fourth martyrdom: When he was weary of torturing him, he sent him back to the city of Kiräs, where he had been raised, to its governor, and the latter subjected him to great torture.
ኃምስ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡፤ ወሰድዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዱድያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወረከበ ፡ በኀ ቤሁ፤ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ተአማንያነ ፡ ወናዘዞሙ ፡ ብዙኃ እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ በነገሩ ፤ ወጸጋ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ሳድስ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለመክስምያኖስ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ወሰድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ ጋኒከላኦስ ፡ ወለብዙኃን ፣ ሰማዕታት ፤ ወኩነንዎ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ!" ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ።
Fifth martyrdom: Then, he was led to the emperor Dudyänos (= Diocletian?); he found many confessors with him; he consoled them greatly, for the saint was accomplished in his speech and the grace of God (was) upon him. (The emperor) subjected him to great torture. Sixth martyrdom by the hand of Maksemyänos: When he was led there, he found the saint Gänikalä'os (= Agathange) and many martyrs. He was subjected to great torture with them.
ሳብዕ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለሉቅያኖስ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ መተረ ፣ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ጻ አክሊለ ፡ 'በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት-፤ ። ወመጽአት ፡ ብእሲት። ፣፡ መሃይምንት ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ሶፍያ ፣ ወነሥአት ፡፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወገነዘቶ ፡ ወቀበረቶ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አካውህ፥ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ፍዩም ፡ እምሀገር ፡ "እንተ ፣ ትሰመይ ፡ ስማ ፡ በማይ፣ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ፣ ዓቢየ ፡ በምንኩስናሁ ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ወአኃዞ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ በሣዕርተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ወአሰሮ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወአምሐሎ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ፍትሐኒ ፡ ወሰደዶ፥ ፡ ቅዱስ፤" ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጢስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዐውደ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ 'በጊዜ ፡ ስደት፥ ፡ ወከልሐ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ወኩነንዎ፥ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነ ሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፤ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወእምሥጋሁሩ‹" ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ በደብረ ፤ ነቅሉን፤ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡፤ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት ።
Seventh martyrdom by the hand of Luqyänos (= Lucian): (The latter) tortured him greatly, then cut off his head with a sword, and (the saint) received seven crowns in the kingdom of heaven. A believing woman by the name of Sofyä (= Sophia) came, took the body of the saint, shrouded it, and buried it. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also is the saint abbä 'Akäweh (= Kaou), monk and martyr. This saint was from the region of Feyum (= Fayoum), from a city called Bamäy (= Bâmouyah). He waged a great (spiritual) combat in his monastic state. Satan appeared to him openly: the saint seized Satan by the hair, bound him, and tormented him greatly. Satan adjured the saint and said to him: "By the passion of Christ, release me." The saint drove him away and he was like smoke. After that, the saint went to the tribunal at the time of the persecution, he cried out and said: "I am a Christian openly." He was subjected to great torture; after that, his head was cut off with a sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Even today, miracles and numerous wonders appear from his body in the monastery of Naqlun (= Neklône). May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ፳፻ ማኅበራነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አካውሕ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also became martyrs the eight hundred companions of the saint 'Akäweh. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ማኅው ፡ አይሁዳዊ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስራኤላዊ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ወልህቀ ፡ በሃይማኖተ” ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወተም ሀረ ፡ መጻሕፍቲሆሙ ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንዘ ፡
[On this day also died the saint Yosëf (= Joseph) son of Mähew the Jew. This saint was an Israelite, he grew up in the faith of the Jews and learned their books. One day, he saw children of Christians learning books; he asked his

mother to put him with them. When they were together, he learned the books of the Church and the faith of Christ entered his heart. One day, he met a Christian adolescent, (the latter) loved him greatly and taught him the law of the Christians. He led him to the church at the time of the Eucharistic consecration and the saint Yosëf received the holy mystery. The next day, the reader and the chanters gathered with Yosëf, arranged their clothes in the likeness of a tent and veils, brought bread from the house of their parents, and consecrated a patriarch and bishops while performing the (liturgical) service and playing; they accomplished the sacrament of the eucharist and, having gone into the house of the priest their master, they ate, drank, and remained thus for three days. Then, when the father of Yosëf learned (of this), he was filled with anger, he bound his son's (arms) behind his back, brought him into a tavern whose (oven?) was full of fire, threw him into it, and closed the door of the oven on him. The angel of God extinguished the fire and cooled it. As she could not find her son, his (= Yosëf's) mother wept, lamented, and did not know what to do. Seven days and seven nights later, Yohannes (= John) learned the story of Yosëf, namely that he was in a furnace of fire; he came with his companions and they wept over him, crying and calling him by his name. Yosëf said to them: "Do not weep, my brothers, for I am alive thanks to the help of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, mother of our Lord Jesus-Christ. She stole me from the furnace of fire with her garment." Having heard (this), (the children) told it to the patriarch. Having come with the priests and with the Gospel, the cross, and the censer, (the latter) went to the oven, said a prayer, brought Yosëf out of the oven intact, and delivered him from his bonds. Yosëf asked for holy Christian baptism and the patriarch baptized him with holy Christian baptism. Learning (of this), his father, Mähew, fell at the feet of the patriarch, believed in Christ, and was baptized with the people of his house.

Yosëf remained fasting every seven days until he was twenty-eight years old, having entered his (monastic) cell. Then, when he knew that his death was near, he entered the church of the saint Giyorgis (= George) the martyr, stood before the effigy of our Lady, the holy Mary twice-virgin, mother of God, and prayed to her to intercede for him with her beloved Son. She said to him from inside the effigy: "Rejoice, Yosëf." On Tuesday, at the time of the third hour, he died. May his holy blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለታቤላ?" ፡ ወለጅደቂቅ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ባረከ ፡ እግዚእነ ፤ ኅብስተ ፡፤ ወኅዳጠ ፡ ዓሣ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ በወንጌል ። ሣሀሉ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also (is) the commemoration of Täbëlä and the three children. May their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever.] [On this day also our Lord blessed the seven loaves and a little fish, as it is said in the Gospel. May his mercy be with us, amen.]

On this day also (is) a sermon for you on the ordinance of the (Eucharistic) mystery. For he had pity on you, redeemed you, saved you, and made you chosen and pure for himself, (you) who have trusted in Christ Jesus, have heard the truthful word and the teaching of life, and have believed in the Son of God. Then, he showed you the light of his glory and marked you with the Holy Spirit while you were dead through your sin, having previously walked with it in the lifestyle of the ordinance of this world and having served those who are not gods, the work of your hands, according to what the prophet says: "The gods of the nations (are) of gold and silver, the work of human hands." He says again, once more: "The gods of the nations are demons. As for God, he made the heavens, faith and good (are) before him." To this faith then and (this) good (which are) before him, he has called you in the richness of his mercy and the greatness of his love. He has given you to obtain the forgiveness of sin through his holy baptism by the Holy Spirit and in the holy Church, and so that you might become children of God and children of the Church, to strengthen you by the power of the Holy Spirit and that (the latter) might dwell in your heart through the knowledge of the right faith so that you might be able to receive the holy mystery of Christ, for the forgiveness of sin and the renewal of the soul, the body, and the spirit, and so that it might be for you good health, joy, blessing, and life so that you might know God who saved you from the power of darkness and useless idols and imparted to you to be blessed.

As the prophet says about you: "Blessed are those whose sin is forgiven and those to whom (God) does not impute all their faults! Blessed is the man to whom God does not count his sin!" Do you hear how the holy prophet declares you blessed regarding the manner in which your sin has been forgiven you, you to whom (God) has not imputed your fault?

‘Isäyeyäs (= Isaiah) the prophet has also prophesied about you, to (you) who believe in Christ and turn to him, and (about) those (who are) from among the Jews and those (who are) from among the pagan nations, he said: "The land of Zablon (= Zebulun) and the land of Neftälèm (= Naphtali) and the way of the sea and the beyond of the edge of the Yordänos (= the Jordan) and Galilà (= Galilee) of the nations, which dwelt in darkness have seen a great light; for those who dwelt in the land of obscure death, a great light has risen." Do you understand what the prophet said to you: that you have seen a great light and that you have come out of the darkness and (the) obscurity of death? A great light has risen for you, just as Zakäryäs (= Zechariah) the chief of the apostles, prophet and martyr, prophesied about Christ; he said: "It has risen to show its

light to those who dwell in darkness and the obscurity of death, to direct our feet toward a path of peace." Now that you have found such salvation and honor in Jesus-Christ, put off from yourselves your old way of being, the old man who is corrupted by the desire of sin. Renew the spirit of your heart and clothe yourselves in Christ Jesus; for, inside you, Christ dwells in truth, the right faith, and gentleness. Reject impurity and speak the truth with your neighbor. Let him who stole steal no more; let him work and toil with his hands to give alms and for the needs of his food. Let no ugly word come out of your mouth but (only) a good one. Do not offend the Holy Spirit with whom you were marked when you were saved. Put off from yourselves all bitterness, fury, anger, murmuring, insult, scandal, blasphemy of the impious, and worship of idols. Let one not hear against you (the accusations of) impurity and greed. Do not touch any impurity - that which is not pure - when you go to church and have turned your gaze from everything that is impure to go to the house of God. Do not swear falsely, keep yourselves from the Jews, the magicians, and the idolaters; keep away from all of them, do not bind yourselves in friendship with them, and do not join yourselves to them, so that they do not drag you into their faults and do not bring you disgust in their impurity. Do not resemble them in anything, as the apostle says: "Do not unite yourselves with those whose works have no fruit, they are entirely darkness." About you, to (you) who believe rightly in Christ, the apostle says: "You (are) all children of light in our Lord; conduct yourselves as children of light, for the fruit of the light (is) every good work."
ለብርሃን ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወአክብሩ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ እለ ፡ እምቅድሜክሙ ፡ ወይልህቁቅሙ ፡ በልደተ ፡፤ ጥም ቀት ። እመሂ ፡ በልደተ ፤ መዋዕል ፡ ዘይንእሱክሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ አክብር ዎሙ ። በከመ ፡ይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ አክብሩ ፡ ዘይልህቁክሙ ፡ ወተአዘዙ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ወዕቀቡ፣፤ ዘመሀሩክሙ ፡ ወዘበቃልሂ ፡ ወዘበመጽሐፍሂ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ትገ ብሩ ፡ አኃዊነ ፡ ንነግረክሙ ፡ ዑቅዎሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይጻምዉ ፡ በውስቴትክሙ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይሜህሩክሙ ። አክብርዎሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ አፍቅርዎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፤ ምግባሮሙ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወእለሰ ፡ ይትለአኩ ፡ ሠናየ ፣ ቀሲሳን ፣ ምክዕቢተ ፡ ክብር ፤ ይደልዎሙ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ እለ ፡ ይሰርሑ ፡ በቃል ፤ ወበምህሮ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ቀኖናሂ ፡ ዘሕግ ፡፤ ለእመቦ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወቀሲስ ፡ ዘያሰትት ፡ ሕዝበ ፤ ወኢይሜህር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ሥርዑ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዘይትኬነኑ ። ወበእንተዝ ፤ ንጽሐቅ ፣፤ ምህሮተክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንድኃን ፡ እምኩነኔኔ ፡ ወኢኮነሰ ፡ በተጻ
Honor the Christians who (became so) before you and are older than you by the birth of baptism; even if, by natural age, they are younger than you, honor them before the Christians, as the apostle says. "Blessed are those who are older than you, obey them and observe what they have taught you both by word and by writing." As the apostle says: "According to how you act, (we) your brothers, we say to you: Observe those who toil among you and those who are at your head because of Christ and teach you. Honor them greatly, love them because of their works." He says again: "The priests who exercise their ministry well deserve double honor, especially those who toil in the word and teaching." people and does not teach them the worship well, institute for them that they be punished." That is why we teach you with zeal to be preserved from punishment: and (this) is not because we believe ourselves just and out of love for the word; (even) if (this) were not (so), our ministry would oblige us to toil and to work to teach the commandment of God. We pray, we ask God, and we beseech with all our heart that he make us capable of teaching and toiling, according to what has been given to us, so that we may not be negligent nor lazy nor inattentive so as not to be punished. If we are negligent, we will be shown punishment and if we teach, we will be shown a reward. We too, let us teach and toil according to what we have been made capable of so that (God) may reward us with the beautiful reward that he has prepared for those who love him, with compassion, mercy, and with his love for men, not because of our perfect ministry, but by his mercy. It is about us that the Lord made a parable in the Gospel about those who had received talents - he who had received five and he who had received two; (he says) that, when they had doubled them, they had made them bear fruit through teaching. Our Savior said to them: "Enter into the joy of your master." (Of) him who received one and buried it, he said (that he had

acted) with ignorance and absence of eloquence. Now, meditate, you too, do with zeal what we have taught you. May God make us capable, we too, of teaching you the right faith. After you have arrived at the church with reflection and calm and with a pure soul, enter and pray; begin with the prayer of the Gospel; after that, address all your prayers to God. Respond to those whom you find chanting psalms in the church to be associated with those who glorify God and to be one with them. When the priest prays and says: "Yereni päsi", respond to him and say to him: "Qétopäne wemâtusi". (You) who are not capable of saying that, say "with your spirit" so that the blessing with which he has blessed you may reach you. If you have not responded to him, have remained silent, and have not accepted the peace that he has given you, his peace will return to him, as our Lord says: "If there is a son of peace here, let your peace rest upon him. If not, let your peace return to you." Indeed, the translation of Yerini päs is "peace be with you all". Consequently, do not neglect to respond to him (= the priest) to acquire the peace. When each psalm is completed and one says "Glory to the Father, to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit!", when the priest says "Now, and always, and for ever and ever, amen", immediately, say diligently "amen". Indeed, what the priest says: "Now, and always, and for ever and ever, amen" is not fortuitous and is not vain, this word is not small but very great. In

fact, (it is) the explanation of the mystery that this word "Now, and always, and for ever and ever, amen"; it means this: all the glorification that is conferred upon the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit. So, having read the doxology of the psalm, when one has finished and after one has been silent, that the priest repeats "Now, and always, and for ever and ever, amen" is all this glorification that is made and conferred upon the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit so that it may be forever, be made continuously, and exist now, and always, and for ever and ever, amen. This means for the priest who speaks thus that the glory of God (is) for ever and ever, amen. May it be now, and always, and for ever and ever, amen. What he says, "amen", is it not necessary that you say it with joy, that you say now with all diligence "amen"? Then one will read Päwlos (= Paul), the Apostle, and the Acts of the apostles, lend an ear with intelligence to observe every good action that one has taught you. When the Gospel is read first, respond to it without negligence and without laziness, (likewise,) when (one is) at the responses of the psalms and when (one is) at the alleluia, for then great (is) the glorification. Of the alleluia also the explanation (is) important, for "alleluia" means "we praise him who is God"; thus the apostles explained it in the Sinodos. Consequently, do not neglect to respond "alleluia" to praise him who is God. When the Gospel is read, lend an ear with intelligence to hear the story of our Lord while bowing your head before the holy Gospel, glory of Jesus-Christ. When the catechumens have been dismissed, (and) there remain (only) the Christians and (the glorification of) the hosts of angels is read, respond to it with joy to join in the glorification of the hosts of angels who are standing, glorifying, and surrounding the mystery of Christ. Then the priests offer the holy eucharist of Christ which is offered as a sacrifice to God so that the sins of all those who approach him in uprightness (and) in faith with beautiful actions may be forgiven. When one says: "Greet one another with a holy kiss", then greet one another with a pure heart, having forgiven all the faults that you have reproached your neighbor for.

When the deacon who presents (the eucharist) says: "Let them stand in order, look toward the east and we look", immediately, do as one (has ordered you, stand in order and look toward the east with the eyes of your body and your spirit while meditating and understanding that it is necessary that you stand before God, just as 'Élyäs (= Elijah) the prophet says: "God of Israel, valiant God before whom I stand." He says this while being on earth like us; he says "I stand before him" to teach us and he

ባን ፡ ክቡር፤"። ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሀሎነ ፤ ንቀውም ። ሶቤሃ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ በፍርሀት ፡ ወበረዓድ ፡ ወዑቃቤ ፡ ንቁም ፡ በከመ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወይቤ ፡ ተቀነዩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በፍርሀት ። ወተሐሠዩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በረዓድ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እስመ ፣ በርቱዕ ፡ ቆማ ፡ እገሪየ ። በማኅበር ፡ እባርከከ ፡ እግዚኦ ።
gives us the intelligence so that we may know that, when we enter where the glorious offering is made, we stand before God. So, because of that, let us stand in fear, in trembling, and (in) attention according to the word of the prophet; (the latter) says: "Serve God in fear and rejoice in him in trembling." He says again: "My feet stand in the straight (path); in the assembly, I will bless you,
ወሶበ ፡ ዘይቄርብ፤" ፣ እመሂ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ቀሲስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩልክሙ ። ወተሰጠውዎ ፡ አንትሙሂ ፡ ወበልዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መንፈስከ ። ወእ መሰ ፡ ኢተሰጠውክምዎ ፡ ጌጋየ ፡ ይከውነክሙ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ኢመፍትው ፡ የሀሉ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌክሙ ፡ ይመስል ። እስመ ፡ ለዘጸለየ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ካህን ፡ ወባረከ ክሙ ፡ በስመ ፡ አምላክክሙ ፡ ወኢያውሣእክምዎ ፡ በረከተ ፡ አላ ፡ ተጸመምክምዎ ። ወእመሰ ፡ ትብልዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መንፈስከ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ይከውነክሙ ፡ ወበረከተሂ ፡ ዘጸለየ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ይበጽሐክሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ሙሴ፣ ወአሮን ፤ በክህነቶሙ ። ወሳሙኤልኒ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፣ ይጹውዑ ፡ ስሞ ። ይጹውዕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፤ ውእቱኒ ፣ ይሠጠዎሙ ፡ ትሰምዑኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ይሰጠዎሙ ፡ ለካህናቲሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይጹውዕዎ ። እፎ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ መፍትው ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ትሰጠውዎሙ፤" ፥ ለካህናት ፡ ሶበ ፤ ይባርኩክሙ ።
Lord." When he who offers - bishop or priest - (says): "God (be) with you all", you, respond to him and say to him "(and) with your spirit". If you do not respond to him, it will be a fault for you and it will seem thus (that) it is not fitting that God be with you. For, to the priest who has prayed for you and blessed you in the name of your God, you have not returned a blessing, but you have turned a deaf ear to him. If you say to him "with your spirit", that is good for you and the blessing that he has called for you by his prayer will reach you, as the Scripture says: "Musë (= Moses) and 'Aron (= Aaron) are holy by their priesthood." Sämu'ël (= Samuel) also (was) with those who called upon his name; they called upon God, he answered them. Do you hear that God himself answers his priests when they call upon him? How much more is it necessary that you, you answer the priests when they bless you!

When the priest says again: "Lift up your hearts", respond and say: "We have (them) with God" while lifting your heart to God and thinking of Christ who is seated at the right hand of God, for what you say to him "we have (them) with God" (means): it is Christ himself that we have with God, our savior, helper, and defender, at the moment when we are in peril with our sin and (when) Satan accuses us with this sin which is ours with which he himself has seduced us. Then, he himself has become our accuser to bring upon man the mortal punishment and pain, as the apostle says: "Desire, once it has conceived, gives birth to sin and sin, when it has been committed, brings death." When we had committed this sin by which (Satan) brought punishment and death, then our Savior Christ pleaded for us so that we might be saved from death and punishment, as the apostle says: "Christ who died, has risen from the dead and is seated at the right hand of God, he will plead for us." Because of that, it is of him - he, our hope, our grace, and our savior, he, Christ Son of God - that we say "we have (him) with God". So that, there, we lift our heart toward the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, there, we pray and, there, we

meditate and do not inherit our hope and our trust from anyone other than the holy Trinity, they teach us to lift our heart to God. When (the priest) says: "Let us give thanks to God", respond then and say: "It is worthy, just, and fitting." For, in truth, it is worthy, just, and fitting that we give thanks to God at all times, at every hour, every day and every night for so much good that he has done for us. He has lifted our lowliness and has joined his divinity to our flesh. (This) was not regarding his nature; he was not changed into our flesh, but (it was) while his divinity existed, as it existed before he became incarnate; likewise, having become incarnate and made man, he did not abandon his nature, but he joined to himself our flesh, opposed to the spirit and such as he knew (it). It is for something similar that God has acted for us. Is it not fitting that we give him thanks? And (it is) just that we reflect (on the word of the?) priests who say "Let us give thanks to God". As for those who respond to them and say to them: "(It) is worthy and just and is fitting", respond with them: "How much (will we have to be), if we praise, glorify, and give thanks, to produce a thanksgiving for him according to what is fitting to give thanks to his divinity? What word and (what) tongues will one produce to make praises according to his greatness and his excellence? What creature and what power can give him thanks in proportion to his action? But whoever gives him thanks according as he

(can), he accepts it, for (he is) a good God." When (the priest) says: "You who are seated, stand up", it (is) so that we lift our heart, our thought, and our spirit toward God, (that) we not be distracted then by thoughts that (remain) on the earth, but (so that), in prayer and orison, we beseech God to give us his mercy and that we be saved from evil. For what (comes) next, let us then express every prayer that is useful to us. So that you understand that then no one (remains) seated, all stand up, (the priest) having said beforehand: "Stand up in order". By that one knows that he is standing and that no one then is seated, not even the bishops to whom it has been given to sit on the holy seat. When one is (no longer) seated - where there are many people -, let someone make the offering and all those who remain will stand in fear and trembling. Indeed, according to their greatness and their science, they glorify him who made them great. When one says: "Look toward the east", then one calls you (to order) if it has happened that you have been negligent and (if) the thoughts of this world have carried you away. (The priest) will encourage you and will say again following: "Let us look". Then one will make you strong to listen to the word that is said: "For you stand your illustrious animals, the seraphim and the cherubim, each with six wings, hiding their face with two of their wings, with two of their wings hiding their feet, and flying with two of their wings,

who sanctify with all, while we say to you our own glorification: Holy, holy, holy, trine (are you), God of the perfect hosts, the holiness of your glory has filled heaven and earth." Then respond by crying out and say: "Holy, holy, holy, trine (are you) God of the perfect hosts, the holiness of your glory has filled heaven and earth." Pay attention: having said "holy" once, do not cease, (having said it twice, do not cease either, but say three times "holy, holy, holy", saying "holy, holy, holy" three times because of the Trinity. Cease then and say: "Trine (are you), God of the perfect hosts, you of whose glory the holiness has filled heaven and earth." Do not neglect to say that to be united in the glorification with the holy cherubim and
ወሶበሂ ፡ ይትነበብ፤! ፡ አስማተ ፡ እለ ፡ ኖሙ ፡ ይርከቡ ፡ ዕረፍተ ፡ ዘተሠርዓ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይትአመር ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሕያዋን፤፤ ፡ ወምውታን ፡ ወከመ ፡ ተውህበ፤፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስልጣን ፡ በሰማይ ፡ ወበምድር፤፤ ፡ ወበሲኦልሂ ፡ይክል ፡ አዕርፎ፣፤ ነፍሶሙ፤፡ ፣ ለእለ ፡ ይጹዓሩ ፡ በህየ ፡ ለነፍሰ፤።፤ ፣ ጸድቃንሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ እምትፍሥሕ ቶሙ ፡ዲበ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ወዕረፍት ፡ ሀለዉ፤። ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይዜክርዎሙ ፡ ዘይሁቦሙ፤፡ ፡ በረከታተ ፤ ወሕፅነታተ ፡ ዘኢይትነገር ፡ ህብሩ ። ሶቤሃኒ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ጸልዩ ፡ ከመ ፣ ትድኅኑ ፡ እሞተ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወከመ ፡ ትሕየዉ ፡ በምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ በጽሐ ፤ ጽዋዔክሙ፤”፡ ፡ ከመ ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ ትርከቡ ። ወሶበሂ፤ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ጽዋዔ ፡ ለምጽአተ፤፡" ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ ሦርባን ፡ ወይገብእ ፡ መምስጥ ፤ ወይትፋጸይ፤፡ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ በቃል ፡ ቀጢን ። ሶቤሃኒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትቀውሙ ፡ አትሕቱ፤፤።፥ ርእሰክሙ ፣ ወሕሊናክሙ ፡፣ ቅድመ፤3፤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ዘመጽአ ፣ ይቀድሶ፤ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ሞርባን ፡ ወይሬስዮ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፤!ቶ፤ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወጸልዩ፤› ከመ ፡ ይሰረይ ፤ ለክሙ ፣ ኃጢአትክሙ፤፤፤ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ትክሀሉ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተድላሁ፤፤ ፣ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ትትወከፍዎ፤፤' ፣ ለዝንቱ ፡ ፉርባን ፡ ዘቀደሶ ፣ ከመ፤ ፡
seraphim. When the names of those who have fallen asleep are read so that they may find the rest that has been established (for them) and that one may know that Christ (is) the judge of the living and the dead, (that) he has been given sovereignty in heaven, on earth, and in hell, (that) he can give rest to the souls of those who are tormented there, so (that one may know) also that the souls of the just, (reigning?) by the fact of their joy over joy and rest, when (people) make mention of them, give them blessings and nourishment whose aspect is unspeakable, then you, pray to be saved from the death of sin, to live in the practice of good, and so that, if your invocation has (well) arrived, you may find justice. When again the invocation for the coming of the Holy Spirit upon the holy offering takes place, (that) the veil is removed and (that) the deacon whispers in a low voice, then, being standing, bow your head and your thought before the power that has come to sanctify the offering and to make it the blood and the body of Christ. Pray so that your sin may be forgiven and that you may be able, according to what the Holy Spirit has prepared, to receive this eucharist that he has sanctified so that it might be for you life, blessing, and joy.
ይኩንክሙ ፡ ለሕይወት ፡ ወለበረከት፤ ፡ ወለፍሥሓ ። ወሶበ ፡ ካዕበ!" ፡ ይብል ፡ ካሀን ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ይሴባሕ ፡ ዘበኩሉ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ ወቡሩክ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኢየሱስ፤፡ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወመንፈስከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ወሶቤሃኒ ፡ በሉ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ህልው ፡ ወይሄሉ ፡ ለትውልደ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ለዓለመ፣ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወፍካሬሁ፤ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቃላት ፡ ስምዑ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሀለወ ፡ አብ ፣ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ስልጣን ፡ ወመንግሥት ፣ ወኃይል ፡ ወስብሐት ፡ ወለይእቲ ፡ ስላሜ ፡ ይገንዩ ፡ ሱራፌል ፡ ወኪሩቤል ፡ ወኃይላተ ፤ ሰማይ ፡፤ ስልጣናት ፡ ወአጋእዝት ፡ ወሊቃነ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወመላእክት ፡ ያዐርጉ! ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ በዕሪና ፡ ለቅድስት! ፡ ሥላሴ ። ወከማሁ ፡፤ ወልድሂ ፡ ተሰጊዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም፤ ፡ እምቅድስት፤" ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ወፈጺሞ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ፈቃደ አብ፤ጾ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አብ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በየማነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ሀሊዎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሚጦት ፡ ወወልጦት ፡ አላ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይሰጎ ፡ ከማሁመ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ተሰጊዎሂ ፡
When the priest says again: "As you ... he is glorified in all, he who (is) holy in all, and blessed (is) your name with Jesus-Christ and your Holy Spirit", then say: "As it was, is, and will be for the generations of generations, for ever and ever, amen." Listen to the explanation of these words: just as the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit were before the world, in one sovereignty, one (single) royalty, one (single) power, and one (single) glory and (that), before this Trinity, the seraphim, the cherubim, the powers of heaven, the dominations, the lordships, the archangels, and the angels prostrate themselves - they raise a glorification equal to the holy Trinity -, likewise, the Son, having become incarnate in the world of holy Mary the pure Virgin and having accomplished all the will of (his) Father, went up to (his) Father and sat at the right hand of his Father, being without change or transformation, but in the manner in which he was before having become incarnate, thus exactly was he having become incarnate, and equal to the Father and to the Holy Spirit in all power,


[p. 228] {#po.45.p228}
ወዕሩይ ፡፤ ምስለ ፡ አብ ፤ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ስልጣን ፤ ወኃይል ፡ ወመንግ ሥት ። ከመዝ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ፍካሬሁ፤ ፡ ለብሂል ፡ በከመ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ህልው ፡ ወይሄልው ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ለትውልደ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
power, and royalty. Such is the explanation of the words "Just as he was, is, and will be, he will be for the generations of generations, for ever and ever".
ወሰበሂ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትቀውሙ ፡ አትሕቱ ፡ ርእሰክሙ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ኢትስግዱ ፡ ዑቁ ፡ ሶቤሃኒ ፤ አትሕቱ ፡ ርእሰክሙ ፡ ወጸልዩ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ዘይደልወክሙ ፣፤ ተመጠዉ ፤ ቅዱሰ ፡ ምሦርባነ ።
When the deacon says: "While standing, bow your head", take care not to prostrate yourselves, bow then your head, pray so that what is fitting for you may happen (and) receive the holy eucharist.

When again the deacon says: "Let us look", and (that) the bishop or the priest responds and says: "The holy things to the holy", say then: "The Father Holy (is) one, the Son Holy (is) one, one is the Holy Spirit" while confessing the Trinity. The Father true - and he is not (so) by name only -, the Son, true Son who was begotten by the Father in truth - and he is not (so) by name only -, and the Holy Spirit true, who is not foreign to the Father and to the Son, he is the Spirit of truth, the Paraclete, the third illustrious (person), the completion, equal in all to the Father and to the Son - and he is not (so) by name only, but he is (so) truly. However, believing this, say: "One (is) the Father holy, one (is) the Son holy, one is the Holy Spirit." Blessed (will) you (be), then you will have become accomplished in the right faith, then the power of the Trinity will rest upon you and you will become holy through the holy Trinity, according to what was said "the holy things to the holy". Thanks to that, you will be able to receive the holy eucharist which is offered for the forgiveness of sin and for the renewal of your soul, of your body, and of your spirit. After one has spoken to you again, he who has communed - either the bishop or the priest - says: "God (be) with you all"; you, respond to him with joy and say to him: "(and) with your spirit" so that the blessing with which he has blessed you in the name of the Lord your God may reach you. Indeed, the priest has (received) ordination, and the grace of the blessing (which is) in his mouth has been given to him by God. When one says: "Sing and read the praise of the glorious mystery", respond, read, do not remain idle, and read with attention while remembering the word of the prophet who says: "Those who stand in the holy place and let him who reads understand." When you approach the eucharist to receive (it), do not prostrate yourselves then to the ground on your face, but on the knees of your heart and of your spirit; receive (it) while submitting yourselves and praying with a pure heart. When the priest presents the eucharist to you, names (it) for you, and says: "The body of Christ, amen", say it (= "amen") once. When you receive the cup, he will say to you: "The blood of Christ, amen and amen"; say (also "amen and amen") to confess the Trinity. Indeed, it (is) thus that one said "amen", once before the priest and, before the deacon, "amen and

ሜን ። ኢኮነ ፡ ድንቀተ ፡ ዘሠርፅዎ ፡ በዘ ፡ ለሥላሴመ ፡ ይበጽሕ ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአምልኮ ፡ ወከመ ፡ በሥላሴመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ይትገበር ፡ ወበሥላሴ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ይትፈጸም ።
amen". It is not by chance that one instituted that, (it is) so that the amen and the adoration might come to the Trinity only so that, by the Trinity only, every action might be done and (that), by the Trinity, everything might be accomplished.
ወጸሊየክሙሂ ፡ ኢትፃኡ! ፡ ለአቲወ ፤ አብያቲክሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ የዐትዉ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ይጹልዩ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ አኩቴት ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ያነብሩ ፡ እደ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ በበረከት ፡ ትእተዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲክሙ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ትስምዑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰ ብኩ ፡ በበሰናብት ፡ ወበበ ፡ በዓላት ። ወእምከመ ፡ ይቤሉክሙ ፡ እተዉ ፡ በሰላም ፡በሉ፣ አሜን ፡ ባርኩነ ፡ ወእተዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲክሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ትበውኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲክሙ ፡ እትወተ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ጸልዩ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ለነቢር ፤ ውስተ ፡ ቤትክሙ ። ወሶበ፤ ፡ ትበልዑ ፡ ወትሰትዩ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘትገብሩ ፡ ገቢረክሙ ፡ ጸልዩ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ እመ ፡ ትፈትዉ ፡ ትድኃኑ፣0፣ እመንሱት ። ወተጸመዱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ወኢትስርቁ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲ ያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሕፅንክሙ ፡ በነቅዓ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘይፈለፍል ፡ ወይውሕዝ ፡ እምአጥባ ቲሃ ፡ ክቡራት ፡ ወቅዱሳት ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ወጽድቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ይክፍል ክሙ ፡ ለክሙሂ ፡ ወለነሂ ፤ ወንትፌሣሕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወንበል ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለአብ ፤ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ ወዘልፈኒ ፡ ወለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Having prayed, do not go out to return to your houses before one has put away the sacred vessels, nor before one has pronounced the prayer of thanksgiving, nor before one has laid hands on you so that you may return to your houses with a blessing and so that you may hear preaching every Sunday and every feast. When one has said to you: "Return in peace", say: "Amen, bless us" and return to your houses. When you enter your houses, upon returning from church, say the prayer of the Gospel to remain in your house. When you eat and drink and while doing everything that you do, say the prayer of the Gospel if you desire to be delivered from temptation. Submit yourselves to the Church and do not steal (anything) from the Church so that she may nourish you from the source of life that gushes and flows from her glorious and holy breasts which are the law and justice, so that, through her, (God) may give you a share, to you as well as to us, and we will rejoice in the kingdom of heaven. Let us say: "Glory to the Father, to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now, and always, and for ever and ever!"

### READING OF THE 29 TERR
አመ ፡ ፳፪8ለጥር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ አክሳኒ፤ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሃ ፣ ፈላሲት ። ዛቲ ፤ ቅድስት" ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምውሉደ ፡ ክቡራን ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወብዑላኒሃ ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዘእንበሌሃ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ መስተጋድልት ፡ እምንእሳ ፡ ትጸውም ፡ ወትጹሊ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ወትረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ?" ፡ ደናግል ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወትጸመድ ፡ ምስሌሆን ። ወዘያመጽኡ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ "ኮነት ፤ ትሁቦሙ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ ወትበልዕ ፡ እምሲሳየ ፡ ደናግል ። "ወኮነት ፡ ወትረ!" ፡ ታነብብ ፡ ውስተ፤፤ ፡ ዜናሆሙ፤" ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ወትስእሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተሳትፋ ፡ ምስሌሆን ። ወፈኃርዋ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ለ8እምልሂ ቃነ ፡ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወሐለዩ ፡ 'ላቲ ፡ አልባሳት ፡ ክቡራት ፡ ወስርግዋት ፡ ብዙኃት፤ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሰብሳባ ፡ ወከብካባ!፤ ፣ ትቤላ ፡ ለእማ ፡ አእምሪ፤ ፡ እምየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ እምድኅረ፤ ፡ አስተዋሰብክሙኒ ፤ ኢይትከሀለኒ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ደናግል ። ወአንሰ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ኀቤ ሆን ፡ ወእትአምፕን፤ ፡ ወእትመየጥ! ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወትቤላ ፡ እማ ፡ ሖሪ ፡ ወለትየ ፣ ወኢትጐንድዬ፥‹ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the saint 'Aksäni (= Xeni) which, translated, (means) "stranger". This saint was among the children of notables and rich people of the city of Romë (= Rome or Constantinople); (her parents) had only her. She was a (spiritual) combatant from her childhood; she fasted and prayed continuously, night and day; she went continuously into a monastery of virgins of the city of Romë and devoted herself to devotion with them. She gave to the poor and the needy the food that one brought her from her father's house and ate the food of the virgins. She read continuously the story of the monks and asked God to associate her with them (= the virgins). Her parents betrothed her to one of the chiefs of the city of Romë and busied themselves with (procuring) for him many precious and ornate clothes, gold, and silver. When the day of her marriage and her wedding arrived, ('Aksäni) said to her mother: "Know, my mother, that, after you have married me, it will not be possible for me to go right away to the monastery of the virgins; I want to go to them and greet them, I will return quickly." Her mother said to her: "Go, my daughter, and do not delay."


[p. 232] {#po.45.p232}
"ወሶበ ፡ ትቤላ ፡ እማ?! ፡ ዘንተ። ፡ ነሥአት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ስርጐታ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ፤ ወአልባሳተ ፡'ወነሥአት ፡ ምስሌሃ ፪አእማተ ፡ ወሖረት" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወረከ በት ፡ ሐመረ ፡እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደሴተ ፡ ቆጵሮስ ፡ ወተጽዕነት ፡ ምስሌሆሙ። ፣ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወበጽሐት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደሴተ ፡ ቆጵሮስ ፡ ወሰመየት?'” ፡ ስማ ፡ አክሳኒ ፣ ዘበትርጓሜሃ ፣ ፈላሲት ። 'ወተጋብአት ፡ በቅዱስ ፡ ኤኢፋንዮስ ፡፤ ወአጠየቀቶ ፡ ኩሎ" ፡ ነገራ ። ወፈነዋ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኤኢፋንዮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ ። ወሶበ ፤ በጽሐት ፡፣ ኅበ ፡ ሀየ ፤ አስተርአያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ በን ዋም ፡ ወመሀራ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ 'ዘይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትግበር፥፤ ፡ ወነገራ ፡ ስሞ ። ወበሳኒታሁ ፡ ተጋብአት ፡ በቅዱስ ፡ ቴዎፊሎስ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወቄረጸት ፡ ስእርተ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ወአልበሳ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወጠት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔሃ ፣፡ እምወርቅ ፡ ወእምብሩር ፡፤ ወአልባሳትቶ፤ ፡ ወሐነጸት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ዲያቆናት ። ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ላቲ ፡ አባ ፡ ቴዎፊሎስ?ቶ" ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ እምሊሂቃነ ፡ ደናግል ፡፤ መነኮሳዊያት ፡ ወሐደራ ፡፤ ኩሎን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፤ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወተጋደለት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ 'ወኢኮነት ፡ ትጥዕም፤ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምዘ ፡ አብሰልዎ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣፤ ኅብስት ፡ ወኢኮነት ፡ ትበልዕ ፡ ካልኣነ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ብቄላተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትነ ውም ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ መንጸፍ ። ወነበረት ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትጋደል ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ገድል ፡ ሠናይ፥" ፡ ወገቢረ፤ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፳ዓመት ፡ ወዘይፈደ
When her mother told her this, (‘Aksäni) took all her gold and silver jewelry and clothing, took two maidservants with her, went to the sea, and found a boat going to the island of Qopros (= Cyprus). She embarked with them (= the sailors) on this boat and arrived at the island of Qopros. She gave herself the name 'Aksäni which, translated, (means) "stranger." She went to the holy 'Epifänyos (= Epiphanius) and told him her whole story. The holy 'Epifänyos sent her to the city of Alexandria. When she arrived there, the apostle Päwlos (= Paul) appeared to her in (her) sleep, taught her everything she ought to do, and told her his name. The next day, she went to the holy Téwofilos (= Theophilus), the patriarch; she cut her hair, and he clothed her in the monastic habit. She sold everything she had with her—gold, silver, and clothing—and built a church in the name of the holy 'Estifänos (= Stephen) the archdeacon. Abbä Tewofilos gathered many of the principal monastic virgins to her, and they all dwelt in that church. This saint waged a great (spiritual) combat; she tasted nothing that had been cooked by fire except bread, and she never ate anything other than vegetables of the earth; she slept on the ground without a mat. She remained thus, waging this beautiful (spiritual) combat and practicing justice, for twenty years and more. Then she had a small illness and died.
ፍድ= ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሐመት ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወአዕረፈት ። ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል+ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዕረፍታ ፡ ዘይኤምር ፡ 'በእንተ ፡ ዘረከበት ፡ እምጸጋ፥ ፡ ሰማያዊ ። ወውእቱኬ፥ እስመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍታ ፣ ርእዩ ፤ በመንፈቀ ፡ መዓልት ፡ 'መስቀለ ፡ ብሩሀ ፡ ወብርሃኑ ፡ 'ከደና ፡ ለብርሃነ" ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወየዓውድዎ ፡ ከዋክብተ፤ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ 'ከመ ፡ አክሊል። ። ወኮነ ፡ ይበርህ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንበሩ ፤ ሥጋሃ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሆን ፡ ለደናግል ፡፤ ወተጋብኡቶ፤ ፡ "ወአእመሩ ፡ ሕዝብ ፤ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በህየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተናገራ ፡ እሎንቱ ፡ ፪ አእማት ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣፤ ወለኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፤ ገድላ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እምጥንታ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ፥ ፡ ዕረፍታ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ወለጠት ፡ ስማ ፡ ወሰመየት ፡ 'ስማ ፡ እክሳኒ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ አምሐለቶን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅብኣ ፣ ምሥጢራቶ ፡ ወይስምያሃ ፡ እኅቶን ፡ ወአኮ ፡ እግዝእቶን ። ወአንከረ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ጳጳሳት ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ 'ወጸሐፉ ፡ ገድላ" ፡ እምጥንቱ ፡ እስከ፣ ተፍጻሜቱ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
God the Most High caused to appear on the day of her death (a sign) which showed what she had obtained of heavenly grace; it is (the following): at the moment of her death, people saw in the middle of the day a luminous cross, its light eclipsed the light of the sun, and stars of light surrounded it like a crown. It shone thus until the body of the saint had been placed with the bodies of the virgins; the people who were there gathered and knew that this (cross) had appeared because of her (= 'Aksäni). Then the two maidservants told the patriarch and all the people the life of this saint from the beginning until the moment of her death, how she had changed her name and given herself the name 'Aksäni, how she had made them swear to hide her secret and to call her their sister and not their mistress. The patriarch and all the people were astonished at this, and her life was written from beginning to end. May her prayer and her blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ፤ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ሰርያኮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ መስተጋድል ። በረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also is the commemoration of the holy Saryäkos (= Cyriacus) the martyr, the (spiritual) combatant. May his blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever]


[p. 234] {#po.45.p234}

[On this day also is the commemoration of the pure women (who were) people of Romë (= Rome): Filmunemä (= Philomena) (and) 'Élyene (= Eliane), and (of) Sarabämon (= Sarapamon), (of) abbä Gabra Näzräwi of Dabra Qäwat and (of the) righteous of Dabra Déguä. May their prayer and their blessing be with our king 'lyäsu for ever and ever.] [On this day also is the commemoration of the feast of the birth of our Lord Jesus Christ, (born) of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary. May his mercy be with our king 'lyäsu for ever and ever.] [On this day also died abbä 'Estifänos (= Stephen), the stranger of the mountain of Feyum (= Fayoum). This saint was a (spiritual) combatant and he sought out the saints who (live) in the desert. One day, while he was wandering in the solitude, he found a head lying there; it was only bone, it had no flesh. When the holy 'Estifänos saw (it), he asked God to teach him what concerned it and what (had been) its faith. Then a voice came from the head which said: "I was a merchant, I made profits with bribes, I did not know almsgiving, and I was not satisfied by the many goods that were with me. One day, while I was on a journey to a distant land, I arrived in a desert place, without water. When the heat of the day became heavy, the camels died, the servants fled, and I remained alone. On the third day, my eyes grew heavy and I heard like (someone) whistling; my spirit went out and I was led to the place of punishment so that I might be repaid according to my actions. When you (asked for it, at that moment I was permitted to tell you my deeds; now, I ask you to ask my Lord for mercy for me.” The saint prayed to God for him so that he would not return to the place of punishment; he prayed and heard a voice that said to him: "I have pity on him because of you." When abbä 'Estifanos heard that, he went into his cave weeping and beating his breast. He remained in a great (spiritual) combat until he died. May God have pity through his prayer on our king 'lyasu for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 30TH OF TERR
ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። አመ ፡ ወለጥር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮና ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ወብፁዓት ፡ ደናግል ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ እለ ፡ እማንቱ ፡ ኢስጢስ ፡ አላሏኢስ ፡ ወአጋቢስ ፡ ወሶፍያ ፡ እሞን፤ ። ዛቲ ፡ ቡርክት" ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምሰ ብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ እምዘመድ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወለደቶን ፡ ለእሎን ፡ ፻ዞአዋልድ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became martyrs the holy and blessed virgins who are Pistis, 'Alapis and 'Agäbis, as well as Sofyä (= Sophia) their mother. This blessed (woman) was from the people of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), of an

ወሰመየቶን ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ አስማት ፡ እለ ፡ በትርጓሜሆን ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወተስፋ ፡ ወፍቅር፣ "ወትርጓሜ ፡ ስመ ፡ እሞን ፡ ጥበብ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቃ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወሰደቶን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወመሀረቶን ፡!'አምልኮ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ወትምህርተ ፡ ቤተ ፤ ክርስቲያን ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ አርድያኖስ” ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብጽሕዎን ፡እንዘ ፡ ይስሕብዎን ፡ በሥዕርተ ፡ርእሶን ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦን ፡ በከመ፣ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወኮነት ፡ እሞን ፡ ትጌሥጾን ፡ ወትምህሮን ፡ ወታስተዔግሦን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽንዓ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወትቤሎን ፡ ተዓገሣ!" ፡ ኦአዋልድየ ፡ ከመ፣ ኢይድክም ፡ ልብክን ፣፤ ወኢትነጽራ፤፤ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ወኢያርሕ ቅን፤ ፡ እምስብሐት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ተዓገሣ" ፡ ኦአዋልድየ ፡ 'እስከ ፤ ትከውና" ፣ ምስለ ፤ መርዓዊክን ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወትበውኣ፤ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከብካብ ፡፤ መንፈሳዊ፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሃ ፡ ለብስቲስ'" ፡ እንተ ፡ ትልህቅ ፡፣ ፲ወ፪ዓመት ፡ ወለህልቢስ፤ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመት፤ ፡ ወለአጋቢስ፤" ፡ 8ዓመት ። ወሶበ ፤ አብጽሕዎን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያቅርብዋ"፤፤ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ለእንተ ፡ ትልህቅ ፣ ብስቲስ" ። ወይቤላ ፡ ስምዒ ፡ እምኔየ፤ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ኣስተዋስበኪ ፡ ለ8እምልሂቃነ ፡ መንግሥትየ ፡ ወእሁበኪ ፡ጸጋ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወስግዲ ፡ ለአጵሎን ፡ ወረገመቶ ፡ 'ወጸረፈት ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዝብጥዋ ፡ በአብትር ፡ ወይምትሩ ፡ እደዊሃ ፡ ወያንድዱ ፡ እሳተ ፤ ታሕተ ፡ ቴገን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ፔሳ ፡ ወመዓረ ፡ ግራ ፡ ወአስፈሊጦስ ፡ ወአፍል ሕዎ ፡ ወይደይዋ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወቆመት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ፣፤ ቴገን ፡እንዘ ፡ ትጴሊ ፡ ወኢለከፈታ ፡ እሳት ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቴገን ፡ዛሕን፣ ወከመ ፡ ጠል ፤ በጊዜ»፥0 ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ 'ነበሩ ፡ በህየ፤፤ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ፥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ 'ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፤ ወነሥ አት ፡ እማ ፤ ሥጋሃ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አቅረቡ ፡ ዳግሚተ ፡ ሀልቢስ ፡ 'ወዘበጥዋ ፡ ወቀሠፍዋ ፡ ቅሥፈተ?" ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቴገን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፈልሕ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ላህቡ ፡ ከመ ፡'ሞር ፡ ካዕበ፥ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርግዋ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወይምትሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ዘንተ፤ ፡ ወነሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወነሥአት። ፡ እማ ፡ ሥጋሃ ። ወኮነት ፡ ትፈርህ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወለታ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትንእስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትደንግፅ ፡ እምኩነኔ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ታጸንዓ ፣ ወታስተዔግሣ፤ ። ወአዘዘ ፤ መኩንን፥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንኩራኩር ፡ ወገብሩ ፡፤ ባቲ ፤ ዘንተ ፡ "ወኮነት ፣፤ ትጸርሕ ፣ ኀበ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጽንዓ ፤ ወያስተዓግሣ ። ወፈነወ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወሰበረ ፡ ውእተ ፡
illustrious family. She gave birth to these three daughters and called them by these names which, translated, (mean) "faith," "hope," and "charity"; the translation of their mother's name (is) "wisdom." When they had grown a little, she took them to the city of Romë (= Rome) and taught them the good worship, the fear of God, and the teaching of the Church. Her story reached the ears of the emperor of Rômë 'Ardyänos (= Hadrian) the impious; (he) ordered them to be brought by dragging them by the hair; they acted toward them according to (what) the emperor had ordered. Their mother exhorted them, instructed them, and urged them to constancy so that they might be firm in the faith of Christ. She said to them: "Be constant, my daughters, so that your heart does not weaken, (that) you do not look at the glory of this transitory world and (that God) does not distance you from the eternal glory. Be constant, my daughters, until you are with your fiancé Christ and you enter with him into the spiritual wedding." The age of Bestis, the eldest, was twelve years, (that) of Helbis eleven years, and (that) of 'Agäbis nine years. When they were brought to the emperor, (he) ordered the eldest, Bestis, to be brought near him; he said to her: "Obey me and I will marry you to one of the greats of my empire and will give you many favors; prostrate yourself before 'Apelon (= Apollo)." She cursed him and insulted him. He ordered her to be struck with sticks, her hands to be cut off, and fire to be lit under a cauldron in which (there was) pitch, wax, and asphalt, to make it boil and to throw her into it; they did this to her. She stood in this cauldron praying; the fire did not touch her at all; in the cauldron, the liquid ceased to boil and (became) like dew at the time of the morning. The people who were there were astonished and praised God. Then, (the emperor) ordered her head to be cut off; they cut off her head with the sword and she received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven; her mother took her body. Then, they brought the second, Halbis, near; they struck her and made her undergo a great scourging. Then they threw her into the cauldron which was boiling, and the brazier became cold again. Then, the emperor ordered her to be taken out and her head to be cut off; they did this to her and she received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven; her mother took her body. She feared that her youngest daughter might be frightened by the torture, and she was strengthening her and exhorting her to constancy. The prince ordered her to be put on a wheel, and they did this to her. She was crying out to our Lord Christ so that he would strengthen her and exhort her to constancy. God sent his angel and (he) broke the wheel. The emperor

መንኩራኩረ፤ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ፥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶነ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወዓተበት ፡ ትእምርተ ፡-መስቀል ፡'ዲበ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ወወገረት" ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ውስተ ፡-እቶነ፡ እሳት ። ወኮነ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጠል ፡ ቄሪር ፡ ወርእዩ ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በህየ ፡ዞ ዕደወ ፡ በአልባስ ፡ ፀዓዳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዓውድዋ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወብዙኃን ፡ አምኑ፣ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርስኑ ፡ አብትርተ ፡ ሐጺን ፡ ውስተ፤ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወይረስይዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ገበዋቲሃ ፡ ወኮነ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያጸንዓ ፡ ወኢለከፈታ ፡ እሳት ፡ ግሙራ ። 'ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በሰ
ordered her to be thrown into a furnace of fire; she made the sign of the cross on her face and threw herself into the furnace of fire. (The fire) then became like dew; those who were there saw three men in white clothes surrounding her and they were greatly astonished. Many believed in our Lord Christ; they cut off their heads and they became martyrs. Then, the emperor ordered iron spits to be heated in the fire and to be driven into her sides; God strengthened her and the fire did not touch her at all. Then, the emperor ordered her head to be cut off with the sword; they cut off her head and she received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven.
ይፍ ፡ 'ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ወነሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወነሥአት ፡ እማ ፡ ሥጋሆን ፡ ለ፻አዋልዲሃ ፡ ወገነዘቶን ፤፣ ወአብጽሐቶን ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካን ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወነበረት ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ላዕሌሆን ፡፤ ወትስእሎን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስአላ ፡ ኅበ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነሚአ ፡ ነፍሳ ፤ ወተወከፈ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስእ ለታ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ነፍሳ ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወገነዝዋ ፡ ወአንበሩ ፡ ሥጋሃ” ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ አዋልዲሃ ፡ ወቀበርዎን?፤ ። ወንጉሥሰ" ፡ እስመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወነቅዓ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፤ ወሥጋሁ ፤ ይትነተፍ” ፡ እስከ ፡ አዕጽምቲሁ ፡ ያስተርኢ ። እደዊሁ ፡ ተወቅያ” ፡ 'ወእምውስተ ፡ ደሙ ፡ መግል ፡ ወዕጹ ፡ ይወድቅ ፡ 'ወኩልንታሁ ፡ በጸዕር” ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በሞት ፡ እኩይ ፤ 'ወተበቀለ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ እምኔሁ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ደናግል፤ ፡ ቅዱሳት ።
Her mother took the bodies of her three daughters, buried them, and carried them to a place outside the city. She remained weeping over them and asking them to ask God to take her soul; God granted her request and took her soul. Believers came, buried her, placed her body with the bodies of her daughters, and interred them. As for the emperor, as God sent him a grave illness, his eyes filled (with worms) and his body was dislocating so much that his bones appeared, his hands were cut off, from his blood fell pus and worms, his whole being (was) in pain, and he died a bad death. God took vengeance on him because of the holy virgins. May their intercession and their blessing be with us, amen.
ትንብልናሆን ፡ ወበረከቶን ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፤ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀ ገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምፕልቆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ጣወ፪ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በጽሖ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፤ ሲመቱ ፡ ወፈጸመ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ "ወተዓገሠ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ብዙኅ፤" ፡ ወአስመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዓቀበ ፡ መርዔቶ ፡፤ 8ዓመተ" ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጤቅላ ፡ ወ፪ደናግል ፡ እለ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሆን ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ፎላ ። ወአስተዋደ ይዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይበርብሩ ፡ ንዋዮ ። ወበጺሖ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ አስተብቀዖዖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚጥ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ወዓበዮ ። ወለቅዱሳትሰ" ፡ ደናግል ፡ አምጽእዎን ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዞን ፡ ለፀሐይ ፡ ይስግዳ ። ወሶበ ፡ አበ ያሁ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለፎላ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ትበልዕ ፡ ማውታ ፡ ወትሰቲ ፡ ደመ ፡ ኣገብእ ፡
On this day also died the holy father abbä Minäs (= Menas), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarchs, he is the forty-seventh. Great misfortunes befell this father during the time of his charge; he finished his life beautifully and was constant in many misfortunes; he pleased God, guarded his flock for nine years, and died in peace. May God have pity on us through his prayer and may his blessing and his intercession be with us for ever and ever, amen. [On this day also became martyrs the holy Téglà (= Thecla) and the four virgins who (were) with her. There was with them a sinner, a priest by the name of Folä. He was denounced as being rich and (the governor) ordered his goods to be confiscated. Arrived before the governor, he begged him to return his goods, but (the governor) refused him. As for the holy virgins, they were brought to the governor and he ordered them to worship the sun. As they refused (it) to him, he said to Folä the priest: "If you eat a corpse and drink blood, I will return your goods to you." Folä did as he had ordered him and denied Christ. When the governor no longer had a pretext to kill Folä, he said to him: "If you persuade the virgins to deny their faith, I will return your goods to you." Folä reported to the virgins what the
