governor had (made) him say, and the virgins insulted Folä, saying: "Son of Satan and enemy of justice, how (can) you tempt us so that we deny Christ while you are our master!" Having learned their words, the governor ordered them to be beaten with rods; they did not cease to reply to the governor and were not afraid of his torture. The governor said again to Folä: "If you kill the virgins, I will return your goods to you." Hearing (this), (Folä) gave himself courage and went to kill the virgins, for the love of gold filled his heart like Judas Iscariot. The virgins said to this bad shepherd: "How (can) you destroy your sheep for the love of gold! Have we not received from your hand the body and the blood of Christ?" While they were saying these words, he cut off their heads with the sword like a man expert in war. Seeing the madness of Folä the priest, the governor (had) him killed with the sword, and he died and lost his goods. The names of the virgins (are) Téqalä, Märyä (= Mary), Märtä (= Martha), 'Amatä and 'Abyä. May their holy blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኦርኒ ። ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ኮነ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮም ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ መርቅያኖስ ፡ ዘይሰግድ ፡ ለጣዖት ፡ ወእማሰ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፤ ድንግል" ። ወሶበ ፡ ወለድዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሐነጹ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ማኅ ፈደ ፡ ወአብእዋ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ደናግል ፡ እለ ፡ ሥርግዋት፣ በወርቅ ፡ ወበብሩር ፡ ወአዕረገ ፤ ምስሌሃ ፤ 3ወጂጣዖታተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስግድ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወሐተጦመ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ኖኅተ ፡ ወነበረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ ጻዓመተ ፡ ወሂአውራኃ ። ወአሐተ፣፤ ዕለተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አንሥአት ፡ አዕይንቲሃ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሰማይ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወርእ የት ፡ ርግበ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ቄጽለ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ወወገረት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማእድ ። 'ወካዕበ ፡ ተመይጠት ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምዕራብ ፡ ወርእየት ፡ ቋዐ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ወወገረ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማእድ!የ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ርእየት ፡ አንቄ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ አክሊል፤፤ ፡ ወወ ገረ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማእድ ፡ ወወፅአ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ራእይ ፡ አንከረት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፤ መምህራ!፤"። ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ ዘወሀባ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወዜነወቶ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ራእየ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መምሕር ፡ ርግብሰ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወቄጽለ ፡ ዘይትኒ ፡ ማኅተመ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወአንቄኒ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአክሊልኒ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወቋዕሰ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ድኩም ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀለወኪ ፡ ትትኩነኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።
[On this day also the holy 'Orni (= Irene) became a martyr. The father of this saint was an emperor of Rom (= Rome) by the name of Margeyänos (= Marcian) who worshipped idols, but her mother (had been) a Christian virgin. When they had begotten this saint, they built a tower for her; they brought her there with twelve virgins who (were) adorned with gold and silver. (Her father) had eighty-seven idols brought up (into the tower) with her so that she would worship them and sealed the door upon her. She remained seven years and seven months in this tower and, one day, as she lifted her eyes to heaven toward the east, she saw a dove and (there was) in its beak an olive branch; it threw it on the table. She turned again toward the west and saw a raven and (there was) in its beak a serpent; it threw it on the table. She saw again a falcon and (there was) in its beak a crown; it threw it on the table and went away. When the vision was finished, she was astonished and, when her teacher whom her father the emperor had given her came to her, she told him this vision. The teacher said to her: "The dove (is) the wisdom of Rom and the olive branch is the seal of baptism. The falcon is a powerful king and the crown is a beautiful action. But the raven is a weak king, for it falls to you to be tortured for Christ."
ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ልህቀት ፡ ዓርጉ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ፣ ወተናገርዋ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግብረ ፡ አውስቦ ፡ ወትቤሎሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኦርኒ ፡ ጽንሑኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰቡዕ፤ቦ ፡ መዋ ዕል ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየት ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምርሓ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኃረየ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ጽንዒ ፡ ወልበሲ ፡ ወቅንቲ፤!”፡
Then, when she had grown up, her father and mother came up to her and spoke to her of marriage. The saint 'Orni said to them: "Give me seven days of reflection." Then, she prayed to God so that he would indicate to her the path he had chosen. The angel of God came to her and said: "Be strong, clothe yourself and gird yourself with power, for Timotëwos (= Timothy), the disciple of

ኃይለ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤኪ ፣ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ረድእ ፣ ዘጳውሎስ ፤ ወያጠምቀኪ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወትከውኒ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ ሰጢቆ ፡ አረፍተ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወመሐራ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትእ ዛዘ ፡ ወንጌል ፣፤ ወእምዝ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሃ፤ ፡ ወአጥመቃ!' ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወተንሚኣ!‹ዐ ፡ ቅድ ስት ፤ ኦርኒ ፤ ሰበረት ፡ ኩሎ ፡፤ አማልክተ ፡ አቡሃ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሰቡዕ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽኡ፤ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ፤ ወተናገርዋ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወትቤሎሙ ፡፣ ቅድስት ፤ ኦርኒ ፡ ተጸዋዕኩ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወተጠመቁ ፡ በስሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወለቱ ፡ ተመልአ ፡ መዓተ ፡ ወአውረዳ፤' ፡ ስሒቦ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ጽርሕ ፡ ወእማኒ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ትተልዋ ፡ ወትወዲ ፡ ሐመደ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእሳ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ፪አፍራሰ ፡ እለ ፣ እሱራን ፡ በሰናስል ፡ ወይእስርዋ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ኦርኒ!፤ ። ወ፳ፈረስ ፤ አምሠጠ ፡ መቲሮ ፡ ሰናስለ ፡ ወመተረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዘየማን ፡ ለንጉሥ፤፤፤ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ወተንሚኣ፤፤ ፡ ኦርኒ ፡ ወገብረት ፡ ጸሎተ!" ፣ ወአንሥአቶ ፡እሙታን፤፤ ፡ ወሜጠት ፡ እደ፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ሕዝብ፣፣ አምኑ ፤ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤፤ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እደዊሃ ፣ መጠነ ፡ ፻፻፻ ነፍስ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ዳኬዎስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአኃዛ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ኦርኒ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይስቅልዋ ፡ በሥዕርታ ። ወካዕበ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ግብ ፡ እሙቅ ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ ውስቴታ ፤! ፡ አክይስት ፡ ወአቃርብት ፡ ወሞቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ፣፤ በኃይለ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ወካዕበ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይስትርዋ ፡ በመጋዝዕ፤፤ ፡ ወመጋዝዕ ፡ ተሰብረ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዕልው ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዓገታ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአ ኃዛ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ኦርኒ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይቀንውዋ ፡ እደዊሃ ፡ ወእገሪሃ ፡ በመራፍዕት ፡ በሊ ሓት ፡ ወያንብሩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዘባና ፡ ዓበይተ ፡ ፕፃ ፡ ወይእስርዋ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያማስኑ ፤ ሥጋሃ ፡ ወበዝኒ ፡ ኢክህልዋ ፡፤ ወለውእቱኒ ፡ ዕልው ፡ ቀተሎ ፡ መልአከ ፡
Päwlos (= Paul) is going to come to you and is going to baptize you with Christian baptism, and you will become a martyr for the name of Christ." Timotëwos came toward her, entered to her, having broken the walls of the house, and taught her all the commandment of the Gospel. Then, he brought water, prayed over her (= 'Orni), baptized her in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, and left her. Having risen, the saint 'Orni broke all her father's idols. Seven days later, her father and mother came and spoke to her as before. The saint 'Orni said to them: "I have been called to the heavenly king Jesus Christ and I have been baptized in his name.” When the father heard his daughter's words, he was filled with anger. He had her brought down from her upper room by dragging (her), her mother following her and putting ashes on her head. He ordered four horses to be brought which (were) tied with chains and to tie the saint 'Orni. One horse escaped, having broken the chains, and severed the right hand of the emperor; (he) fell and died. Having risen, 'Orni made a prayer, resurrected him from the dead, and restored (his) hand as (it was) before. Seeing (this), the people believed through her intermediary in the name of our Lord Jesus Christ, (they were) thirty thousand souls. Learning (this), Däkëwos (= Decius) the emperor entered this city, (had) the saint 'Orni seized, and ordered her to be suspended by her hair. He ordered again for her to be thrown into a deep pit which (was) full of serpents and scorpions, but all died by the power of God. He ordered again for her to be shredded with saws; the saws broke and the impious emperor died. Learning (this), his son besieged the city, seized the saint 'Orni, and ordered her hands and feet to be nailed with sharp spikes, to put on her back a large (quantity of) sand, and to tie her to four horses so that they would destroy her body. Even with this, they could (do nothing) against her; as for the impious one, the angel of God killed him with a spear of fire.
እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ በኩናት ፡ ዘእሳት ። ወነበረት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኦርኒ ፤ እንዘ ፡ ትፌውስ ፤ ኩሎ፤፤ ፡ ድውያነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምው ታነ ፡ አንሥአት ። ወመጽአ ፡ ራብዕ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሰሚያዖ ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ወአብጽሓ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወአገበራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሥፅ ፡ ለአማልክት ። ወሶበ ፡ አበየቶ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይደይዋ ፣ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወወፅአት ፡ እምውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወረገመቶ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ አምነ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ በክርስቶስ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአኃዛ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ኦርኒ ፡ ወረገዛ ፡ በሹናት ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ወሞተት ። ወእምዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሖር ፡ ወአንሥኣ ፡ ወቦአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትሰ ብክ ፡ በስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተመሥጠት ፡ ውስተ ፡ገነተ-፡ ትፍሥሕት ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጸሓፊሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
The saint 'Orni remained healing all the sick to the point that she resurrected the dead. A fourth emperor arrived, having learned her story; he had her brought to him and (wanted) to force her to sacrifice to idols. As she refused (it) to him, he ordered her to be thrown into the fire; she came out of the fire and cursed the emperor. When he saw this miracle, he himself believed in Christ. The king of Färs (= Persia) arrived in the city; he seized the saint 'Orni, pierced her with the spear that (was) in his hand, and she died. Then, the angel of God came, resurrected her, and she entered the city preaching the name of Christ. A short time later, she was taken up into the garden of joy. May her prayer and her blessing be with his (= of this book) scribe for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጎርጎርዮስ ፡ ተናገሬ ፡ መለኮት ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also is the commemoration of the holy Gorgoryos (= Gregory) the Theologian. May his intercession be with our king 'lyäsu for ever and ever]


[p. 244] {#po.45.p244}
ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ አክርስጦሮስ፤። ፡ መስተጋድል ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መስተጋድል፤፤ ፡ ይነብር ፡ በገዳመ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፣ ወተስእሎ ፡ ፳እመነኮሳት ፣ ምስለ ፡ ሰጊድ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ አክርስጦሮስ፤።። ፡ ግበር ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ወንግረኒ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብረከ ፡ ከመ ፡ እብቋዕ ፣ ቦቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፤ አመ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መንኩስኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ አነ ፡፤ ኮንኩ ፡፤ አሐውር ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ኅበ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ዘቅዱስ ፡ ቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጸሊ ፡ በህየ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ፲ ወቿ፳መዓርግ"፡፡ ፡ ወለለ መዓርግ ፡ እሰግድ ፡ ፻ ስግደታተ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ይጠቅዑ ፡ መጥቅዓ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣፤ ወእፌጽም ፡ ቀኖናየ ፡ ምስለ ፤ አኃው፤ ፡ ወነበ ርኩ ፡ በዘከመዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ ፲ ዓመተ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ እምለያልይ ፡ እምድኅረ ፤፡ ፡ ጸሎትየ ፡ ዘልማድ ፡ ተፄወወኒ ፡ ሕሊናየ ፡ ወርኢኩ ፡ ፪እደወ ፡ በአልባስ ፡ ፀዓዳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐትዉ፣ ቀንዲላተ ፡ መንፈቆሙ ፡ ይበርሁ ፡ ወመንፈቆሙ ፡ ይጠፍኡ ። ወእቤሎሙ ፡-ምንትኑ፣፤ ውእቱ ፡ ግብር ። ወይቤሉኒ!፤ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አበው ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘያፈቅር ፡ እሁ ፡ ይበርህ ፤ ቀንዲሉ ። ወእቤሎሙ ፡ ዓይኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀንዲልየ ፡፤ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ተፋቅር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃዊከ ፡ ወንሕነኒ ፡ ናበርሆ ፡ ለቀንዲልከ፤" ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቃሀኩ ፡ ኢረከብኩ ፡
[On this day also died abbä 'Akrestoros (= Christopher), the (spiritual) combatant. This holy (spiritual) combatant dwelt in the desert of Yordänos (= Jordan). A monk questioned him while prostrating himself and said to him: "My father 'Akrestoros, have charity for me and tell me everything that concerns you so that I may draw profit from it." The old man said to him: "My son, at the moment when I became a monk being an adolescent, I went at night to the sanctuary of the holy Téwodosyos (= Theodosius) to pray there; (there were) eighteen steps there and, on each step, I made one hundred prostrations until the trumpet was sounded. Then I went to the church and fulfilled my law with the monks; I remained in this way of acting for ten years. One night, after my usual prayer, my thought was captivated and I saw two men in white clothes lighting lamps; half of them were shining and the (other) half were going out. I said to them: "What (is) this thing?" These fathers said to me: "The lamp of whoever loves his brother shines." I said to them: "What is my lamp?" They said to me: "Have love for your brothers and we will make your lamp shine." When I awoke, I found no one."
መነሂ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ አባ ፡ አስክስሮስ፤፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በህየ ፡ ዓመተ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ። ወመጽአ፤ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ ተመየጥ፣ ውስተ ፡ ምኔትከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ 'አኃዊከ ፡ ወአበዊከ፤የ ። ወእምዝ ፣፡ ተመ ይጠ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ምኔቱ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እመቃብሪሁ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Then, abbä 'Askesros went to the mountain of Sinä (= Sinai) alone and remained there fifty years fighting (spiritually). A voice came to him which said to him: "Return to your monastery to be with your brothers and your fathers." After that, he returned to his monastery and died in peace. Miracles and wonders appeared from his tomb. May God have pity through his prayer on our king 'lyäsu for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ኮኑ ፡ ፲ ወ፻፻፻ ነፍስ ፡ ማኅበራነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኦርኒ ፡ ወተዝካረ ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ እለ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ዴጓ፤፤ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሮሙ፤፤ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። በዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፤ ተዝካራ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ።
[On this day also became martyrs the one hundred and thirty thousand souls, companions of the holy 'Orni, and (there is) the commemoration of the death of the righteous of Dabra Déguä. May their holy blessing be with their dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.] [On this day (there is) the commemoration of our Lady Mary mother of God.]
ተፈጸመ ፡ ወርኃ ፡ ጥር ፡ ወስበሐት ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወላዕሌነ፤ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሣህሉ ፤ ወምሕረቱ ፡ እስከ፤፤ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፣ ወአሜን ።
The month of terr is finished, glory to God! May his mercy and his pity be upon us for ever and ever, amen and amen.


[p. 246] {#po.45.p246}
